TL;DR: 论文提出"潜在价值假说"，解释RLAIF为何有效：预训练在表示空间中编码了人类价值方向，宪法提示激活这些方向用于偏好判断，RLAIF通过优化价值相关方向提升对齐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释RLAIF（从AI反馈的强化学习）为何能通过模型自身的偏好判断实现自我改进，特别是价值学习方面缺乏理论解释。现有实证发现（如拒绝方向、低秩安全子空间、RLAIF缩放行为）需要统一的理论框架。

Method: 提出潜在价值假说，形式化为线性模型：预训练在表示空间编码人类价值方向，宪法提示作为投影算子选择价值相关方向。分析RLAIF在价值对齐、能力缩放和对抗性宪法等方面的理论性质。

Result: RLAIF在宪法激活方向比默认生成方向更相关真实价值时改善对齐；RLAIF质量上限由表示编码价值的能力决定，随模型容量扩展；存在对抗性宪法可激活有害预训练数据编码的反社会价值方向。

Conclusion: 潜在价值假说为RLAIF有效性提供了统一理论解释，连接了预训练价值编码、宪法提示激活和价值对齐改进，解释了生成-判断差距、模型容量依赖和对抗性风险等实证现象。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback (RLAIF) enables language models to improve by training on their own preference judgments, yet no theoretical account explains why this self-improvement seemingly works for value learning. We propose the latent value hypothesis, that pretraining on internet-scale data encodes human values as directions in representation space, and constitutional prompts elicit these latent values into preference judgments. We formalize this intuition under a linear model where the constitution acts as a projection operator selecting value-relevant directions. Our analysis yields several results. RLAIF improves alignment when the constitution-activated direction correlates with true values better than the model's default generation direction thus explaining the generation-judgment gap; the ceiling on RLAIF quality is determined by how well representations encode values, which scales with model capacity; and adversarial constitutions exist that can activate anti-social value directions encoded from harmful pretraining data. Our account unifies scattered empirical findings including the refusal direction, low-rank safety subspaces, and RLAIF scaling behavior.

</details>


### [153] [Breaking the Prototype Bias Loop: Confidence-Aware Federated Contrastive Learning for Highly Imbalanced Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03007)
*Tian-Shuang Wu,Shen-Huan Lyu,Ning Chen,Yi-Xiao He,Bing Tang,Baoliu Ye,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAFedCL通过置信度感知的原型聚合、生成增强和几何一致性正则化，解决联邦对比学习中的原型偏差循环问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决联邦对比学习中存在的原型偏差循环问题：由不平衡数据导致的偏置本地原型被聚合成偏置的全局原型，这些全局原型又作为对比锚点被重复使用，导致误差在通信轮次中累积

Method: 1. 置信度感知聚合机制：利用预测不确定性对高方差本地原型进行降权；2. 少数类生成增强；3. 几何一致性正则化以稳定类间结构；4. 理论分析证明聚合方法能减少估计方差，限制全局原型漂移并确保收敛

Result: 在不同程度的类别不平衡和数据异构性下，CAFedCL在准确性和客户端公平性方面持续优于代表性联邦学习基线方法

Conclusion: CAFedCL通过改进原型聚合机制和增强基于原型的对比对齐，有效打破了原型偏差循环，为联邦对比学习提供了更稳定和公平的解决方案

Abstract: Local class imbalance and data heterogeneity across clients often trap prototype-based federated contrastive learning in a prototype bias loop: biased local prototypes induced by imbalanced data are aggregated into biased global prototypes, which are repeatedly reused as contrastive anchors, accumulating errors across communication rounds. To break this loop, we propose Confidence-Aware Federated Contrastive Learning (CAFedCL), a novel framework that improves the prototype aggregation mechanism and strengthens the contrastive alignment guided by prototypes. CAFedCL employs a confidence-aware aggregation mechanism that leverages predictive uncertainty to downweight high-variance local prototypes. In addition, generative augmentation for minority classes and geometric consistency regularization are integrated to stabilize the structure between classes. From a theoretical perspective, we provide an expectation-based analysis showing that our aggregation reduces estimation variance, thereby bounding global prototype drift and ensuring convergence. Extensive experiments under varying levels of class imbalance and data heterogeneity demonstrate that CAFedCL consistently outperforms representative federated baselines in both accuracy and client fairness.

</details>


### [154] [SEHFS: Structural Entropy-Guided High-Order Correlation Learning for Multi-View Multi-Label Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03022)
*Cheng Peng,Yonghao Li,Wanfu Gao,Jie Wen,Weiping Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEHFS是一种基于结构熵的高阶相关学习多视图多标签特征选择方法，通过编码树量化高阶依赖信息成本，结合信息论与矩阵方法平衡全局与局部优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息论方法存在两个关键挑战：1) 难以学习真实数据中的高阶结构相关性；2) 依赖启发式优化易陷入局部最优。需要一种能有效学习高阶特征相关性并避免局部最优的方法。

Method: 将特征图转换为结构熵最小化的编码树，量化高阶依赖信息成本，学习超越成对相关的高阶特征相关性。采用信息论与矩阵方法融合的新框架，学习共享语义矩阵和视图特定贡献矩阵来重构全局视图矩阵。

Result: 在八个不同领域数据集上的实验和消融研究表明，SEHFS在特征选择方面实现了优越性能，证明了结构熵学习高阶相关性的能力。

Conclusion: SEHFS通过结构熵引导的高阶相关学习和信息论-矩阵融合框架，有效解决了多视图多标签学习中高阶相关性学习和局部最优问题，为特征选择提供了新方法。

Abstract: In recent years, multi-view multi-label learning (MVML) has attracted extensive attention due to its close alignment to real-world scenarios. Information-theoretic methods have gained prominence for learning nonlinear correlations. However, two key challenges persist: first, features in real-world data commonly exhibit high-order structural correlations, but existing information-theoretic methods struggle to learn such correlations; second, commonly relying on heuristic optimization, information-theoretic methods are prone to converging to local optima. To address these two challenges, we propose a novel method called Structural Entropy Guided High-Order Correlation Learning for Multi-View Multi-Label Feature Selection (SEHFS). The core idea of SEHFS is to convert the feature graph into a structural-entropy-minimizing encoding tree, quantifying the information cost of high-order dependencies and thus learning high-order feature correlations beyond pairwise correlations. Specifically, features exhibiting strong high-order redundancy are grouped into a single cluster within the encoding tree, while inter-cluster feaeture correlations are minimized, thereby eliminating redundancy both within and across clusters. Furthermore, a new framework based on the fusion of information theory and matrix methods is adopted, which learns a shared semantic matrix and view-specific contribution matrices to reconstruct a global view matrix, thereby enhancing the information-theoretic method and balancing the global and local optimization. The ability of structural entropy to learn high-order correlations is theoretically established, and and both experiments on eight datasets from various domains and ablation studies demonstrate that SEHFS achieves superior performance in feature selection.

</details>


### [155] [Step-Level Sparse Autoencoder for Reasoning Process Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03031)
*Xuan Yang,Jiayu Liu,Yuhang Lai,Hao Xu,Zhenya Huang,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出步级稀疏自编码器（SSAE），用于从LLM推理步骤中提取稀疏特征，解决现有token级方法与步级信息粒度不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏自编码器（SAE）主要工作在token级别，与捕捉推理方向、语义转换等更关键的步级信息存在粒度不匹配。需要一种能够分析LLM推理步骤中不同方面的工具。

Method: 提出步级稀疏自编码器（SSAE），通过精确控制步特征在其上下文条件下的稀疏性，在步重构中形成信息瓶颈，将增量信息与背景信息分离，并将其解耦为多个稀疏激活维度。

Result: 在多个基础模型和推理任务上的实验显示提取特征的有效性。通过线性探测，可以轻松预测表面信息（如生成长度和首个token分布）以及更复杂的属性（如步骤的正确性和逻辑性）。

Conclusion: 观察表明LLM在生成过程中至少部分了解这些属性，这为LLM的自我验证能力提供了基础。SSAE作为分析工具能够有效解耦LLM推理步骤的不同方面。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved strong complex reasoning capabilities through Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. However, their reasoning patterns remain too complicated to analyze. While Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful tool for interpretability, existing approaches predominantly operate at the token level, creating a granularity mismatch when capturing more critical step-level information, such as reasoning direction and semantic transitions. In this work, we propose step-level sparse autoencoder (SSAE), which serves as an analytical tool to disentangle different aspects of LLMs' reasoning steps into sparse features. Specifically, by precisely controlling the sparsity of a step feature conditioned on its context, we form an information bottleneck in step reconstruction, which splits incremental information from background information and disentangles it into several sparsely activated dimensions. Experiments on multiple base models and reasoning tasks show the effectiveness of the extracted features. By linear probing, we can easily predict surface-level information, such as generation length and first token distribution, as well as more complicated properties, such as the correctness and logicality of the step. These observations indicate that LLMs should already at least partly know about these properties during generation, which provides the foundation for the self-verification ability of LLMs. The code is available at https://github.com/Miaow-Lab/SSAE

</details>


### [156] [IoUCert: Robustness Verification for Anchor-based Object Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03043)
*Benedikt Brückner,Alejandro Mercado,Yanghao Zhang,Panagiotis Kouvaros,Alessio Lomuscio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IoUCert是首个专门为基于锚框的目标检测架构设计的正式鲁棒性验证框架，能够绕过非线性坐标变换的精度损失问题，实现对SSD、YOLOv2、YOLOv3等实际模型的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然形式化鲁棒性验证在图像分类中已取得显著成功，但由于复杂的非线性坐标变换和IoU度量，将其扩展到目标检测仍然非常困难。现有方法在处理基于锚框的目标检测架构时面临瓶颈。

Method: 提出IoUCert框架，专注于单目标场景中的目标定位组件。通过坐标变换绕过非线性框预测函数的精度降低松弛，直接优化锚框偏移量的边界，并开发了新的区间边界传播方法来推导最优IoU边界。

Result: 该方法首次实现了对包括SSD、YOLOv2和YOLOv3变体在内的实际基于锚框模型在各种输入扰动下的鲁棒性验证。

Conclusion: IoUCert成功克服了目标检测中形式化验证的关键瓶颈，为基于锚框的检测架构提供了有效的鲁棒性保证，填补了该领域的重要空白。

Abstract: While formal robustness verification has seen significant success in image classification, scaling these guarantees to object detection remains notoriously difficult due to complex non-linear coordinate transformations and Intersection-over-Union (IoU) metrics. We introduce {\sc \sf IoUCert}, a novel formal verification framework designed specifically to overcome these bottlenecks in foundational anchor-based object detection architectures. Focusing on the object localisation component in single-object settings, we propose a coordinate transformation that enables our algorithm to circumvent precision-degrading relaxations of non-linear box prediction functions. This allows us to optimise bounds directly with respect to the anchor box offsets which enables a novel Interval Bound Propagation method that derives optimal IoU bounds. We demonstrate that our method enables, for the first time, the robustness verification of realistic, anchor-based models including SSD, YOLOv2, and YOLOv3 variants against various input perturbations.

</details>


### [157] [Incremental Graph Construction Enables Robust Spectral Clustering of Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03056)
*Marko Pranjić,Boshko Koloski,Nada Lavrač,Senja Pollak,Marko Robnik-Šikonja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种增量k-NN图构建方法，保证图在任何k值下都连通，解决了标准k-NN图在文本嵌入谱聚类中因不连通导致的退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准k-NN图在文本嵌入谱聚类中容易产生多个不连通分量，特别是在实际使用的稀疏水平（小k值）下，这会导致谱聚类退化和对超参数敏感。

Method: 提出增量k-NN图构建方法：每个新节点连接到其k个最近邻的先前插入节点，这种方法设计上保证图的连通性，并提供了连通性的归纳证明。

Result: 在SentenceTransformer嵌入的谱聚类实验中，该方法在低k值区域（不连通分量普遍存在）优于标准k-NN图，在大k值时与标准k-NN图性能相当。

Conclusion: 增量k-NN图构建方法有效解决了文本嵌入谱聚类中图不连通的问题，特别是在低k值区域，为实际应用提供了更稳定的图构建方案。

Abstract: Neighborhood graphs are a critical but often fragile step in spectral clustering of text embeddings. On realistic text datasets, standard $k$-NN graphs can contain many disconnected components at practical sparsity levels (small $k$), making spectral clustering degenerate and sensitive to hyperparameters. We introduce a simple incremental $k$-NN graph construction that preserves connectivity by design: each new node is linked to its $k$ nearest previously inserted nodes, which guarantees a connected graph for any $k$. We provide an inductive proof of connectedness and discuss implications for incremental updates when new documents arrive. We validate the approach on spectral clustering of SentenceTransformer embeddings using Laplacian eigenmaps across six clustering datasets from the Massive Text Embedding Benchmark.Compared to standard $k$-NN graphs, our method outperforms in the low-$k$ regime where disconnected components are prevalent, and matches standard $k$-NN at larger $k$.

</details>


### [158] [Reinforcement Learning with Symbolic Reward Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03068)
*Thomas Krug,Daniel Neider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出符号奖励机（SRM）及其学习算法QSRM和LSRM，通过符号公式直接处理环境观测，无需人工标签函数，在保持RM性能的同时提高适用性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励机（RM）需要环境提供高层标签信息，这要求用户为每个环境和任务手动创建标签函数，限制了RM在广泛RL框架中的适用性。

Method: 提出符号奖励机（SRM），直接处理环境的标准观测输出，通过符号公式表示的守卫条件来替代标签函数，并开发了QSRM和LSRM学习算法。

Result: SRM方法在评估中优于基线RL方法，并达到与现有RM方法相同的性能，同时遵循广泛使用的环境定义并提供任务的可解释表示。

Conclusion: SRM克服了RM对人工标签函数的依赖，提高了在标准RL框架中的适用性，同时保持了任务表示的可解释性，为稀疏、非马尔可夫奖励任务提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Reward Machines (RMs) are an established mechanism in Reinforcement Learning (RL) to represent and learn sparse, temporally extended tasks with non-Markovian rewards. RMs rely on high-level information in the form of labels that are emitted by the environment alongside the observation. However, this concept requires manual user input for each environment and task. The user has to create a suitable labeling function that computes the labels. These limitations lead to poor applicability in widely adopted RL frameworks. We propose Symbolic Reward Machines (SRMs) together with the learning algorithms QSRM and LSRM to overcome the limitations of RMs. SRMs consume only the standard output of the environment and process the observation directly through guards that are represented by symbolic formulas. In our evaluation, our SRM methods outperform the baseline RL approaches and generate the same results as the existing RM methods. At the same time, our methods adhere to the widely used environment definition and provide interpretable representations of the task to the user.

</details>


### [159] [Why Adam Can Beat SGD: Second-Moment Normalization Yields Sharper Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03099)
*Ruinan Jin,Yingbin Liang,Shaofeng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了Adam优化器中二阶矩归一化的关键作用，通过停止时间/鞅分析在经典有界方差模型下理论上区分了Adam与SGD，首次建立了两种方法高概率收敛行为的理论分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Adam在许多应用中表现出比SGD更快的经验收敛速度，但现有理论给出的保证与SGD基本相当，未能充分解释经验性能差距。本文旨在从理论上解释Adam相对于SGD的优势。

Method: 通过揭示Adam中的关键二阶矩归一化机制，并开发停止时间/鞅分析方法，在经典有界方差模型（二阶矩假设）下对Adam进行理论分析。

Result: 首次建立了Adam与SGD在高概率收敛行为上的理论分离：Adam对置信参数δ的依赖为δ^{-1/2}，而SGD相应的高概率保证至少需要δ^{-1}的依赖。

Conclusion: Adam的二阶矩归一化机制使其在高概率收敛保证上具有理论优势，这解释了其经验性能优于SGD的原因，为理解自适应优化算法的理论性质提供了新视角。

Abstract: Despite Adam demonstrating faster empirical convergence than SGD in many applications, much of the existing theory yields guarantees essentially comparable to those of SGD, leaving the empirical performance gap insufficiently explained. In this paper, we uncover a key second-moment normalization in Adam and develop a stopping-time/martingale analysis that provably distinguishes Adam from SGD under the classical bounded variance model (a second moment assumption). In particular, we establish the first theoretical separation between the high-probability convergence behaviors of the two methods: Adam achieves a $δ^{-1/2}$ dependence on the confidence parameter $δ$, whereas corresponding high-probability guarantee for SGD necessarily incurs at least a $δ^{-1}$ dependence.

</details>


### [160] [Torus embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03135)
*Dan Stowell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的深度嵌入表示方法——环形嵌入，将传统欧几里得空间或超球面嵌入扩展到超环面拓扑结构，以匹配计算机整数溢出的实际硬件特性，为TinyML嵌入式实现提供简单高效的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习嵌入主要使用欧几里得空间或超球面表示，但计算机硬件最基础的数值表示是带溢出的整数，向量化的整数对应的是超环面拓扑结构。这种表示空间与硬件特性的不匹配导致表示容量浪费，需要开发与硬件拓扑匹配的嵌入方法。

Method: 提出了两种策略实现环形嵌入：1）基于归一化的策略，通过简单修改常见深度学习框架来创建具有固有环形拓扑的表示；2）研究了环形嵌入的量化特性。重点展示了归一化策略在训练稳定性和性能方面的优势。

Result: 环形嵌入在训练稳定性和性能方面与标准的超球面L2归一化相当，保持了良好的量化特性。虽然环形嵌入在一般情况下不优于超球面嵌入，但性能相当，同时为TinyML嵌入式实现提供了极其简单的实现路径。

Conclusion: 环形嵌入是一种可行的深度嵌入表示方法，能够匹配计算机硬件的整数溢出特性，为高效的TinyML嵌入式实现提供了新的可能性，扩展了深度学习嵌入的拓扑表示空间。

Abstract: Many data representations are vectors of continuous values. In particular, deep learning embeddings are data-driven representations, typically either unconstrained in Euclidean space, or constrained to a hypersphere. These may also be translated into integer representations (quantised) for efficient large-scale use. However, the fundamental (and most efficient) numeric representation in the overwhelming majority of existing computers is integers with overflow -- and vectors of these integers do not correspond to either of these spaces, but instead to the topology of a (hyper)torus. This mismatch can lead to wasted representation capacity. Here we show that common deep learning frameworks can be adapted, quite simply, to create representations with inherent toroidal topology. We investigate two alternative strategies, demonstrating that a normalisation-based strategy leads to training with desirable stability and performance properties, comparable to a standard hyperspherical L2 normalisation. We also demonstrate that a torus embedding maintains desirable quantisation properties. The torus embedding does not outperform hypersphere embeddings in general, but is comparable, and opens the possibility to train deep embeddings which have an extremely simple pathway to efficient `TinyML' embedded implementation.

</details>


### [161] [Information Routing in Atomistic Foundation Models: How Equivariance Creates Linearly Disentangled Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03155)
*Joshua Steier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出成分投影分解（CPD）方法，通过QR投影从原子尺度基础模型的中间表示中线性移除成分信号，探究几何残差。研究发现不同架构模型在信息解耦方面存在梯度差异：张量积等变架构（MACE）产生线性可访问的几何表示，而手工描述符（ANI-2x）则非线性纠缠相同信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究原子尺度基础模型的中间表示中编码了什么信息，以及这些信息是如何组织的。具体关注成分信号和几何信息在模型表示中的分离程度，以及不同模型架构在信息组织方式上的差异。

Method: 提出成分投影分解（CPD）方法，使用QR投影从学习到的表示中线性移除成分信号，然后探测几何残差。在QM9分子和Materials Project晶体数据集上，对来自五个架构家族的八个模型进行了分析，包括张量积等变架构（MACE）、向量-标量架构（ViSNet）和手工描述符（ANI-2x）等。

Result: 研究发现存在解耦梯度：MACE架构产生的表示在移除成分后几何信息几乎完全线性可访问（HOMO-LUMO间隙的R²=0.782），而ANI-2x手工描述符非线性纠缠相同信息（Ridge回归下R²=-0.792，MLP下R²=+0.784）。MACE通过不可约表示通道路由目标特定信号（偶极子到L=1，HOMO-LUMO间隙到L=0），这种模式在ViSNet的向量-标量架构中未观察到。线性解耦的表示在线性探测下具有更高的样本效率。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，等变架构在线性解耦表示方面具有实际优势，超越了原始预测精度。建议将线性探测作为主要评估指标，因为梯度提升树探测在投影残差上会系统性地夸大性能。线性解耦的表示在线性探测下具有更高的样本效率，这为等变架构的实际应用提供了优势。

Abstract: What do atomistic foundation models encode in their intermediate representations, and how is that information organized? We introduce Composition Projection Decomposition (CPD), which uses QR projection to linearly remove composition signal from learned representations and probes the geometric residual. Across eight models from five architectural families on QM9 molecules and Materials Project crystals, we find a disentanglement gradient: tensor product equivariant architectures (MACE) produce representations where geometry is almost fully linearly accessible after composition removal ($R^2_{\text{geom}} = 0.782$ for HOMO-LUMO gap), while handcrafted descriptors (ANI-2x) entangle the same information nonlinearly ($R^2_{\text{geom}} = -0.792$ under Ridge; $R^2 = +0.784$ under MLP). MACE routes target-specific signal through irreducible representation channels -- dipole to $L = 1$, HOMO-LUMO gap to $L = 0$ -- a pattern not observed in ViSNet's vector-scalar architecture under the same probe. We show that gradient boosted tree probes on projected residuals are systematically inflated, recovering $R^2 = 0.68$--$0.95$ on a purely compositional target, and recommend linear probes as the primary metric. Linearly disentangled representations are more sample-efficient under linear probing, suggesting a practical advantage for equivariant architectures beyond raw prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [162] [Less Noise, Same Certificate: Retain Sensitivity for Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03172)
*Carolin Heinzler,Kasra Malihi,Amartya Sanyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"保留敏感性"概念，通过固定保留数据集R来减少认证机器遗忘中的噪声添加，相比传统差分隐私方法能提供相同证书但噪声更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认证机器遗忘方法通常采用差分隐私技术，基于全局敏感性添加噪声，但这对于遗忘任务过于保守。认证遗忘本质上不需要保护保留数据R的隐私，因此可以定义更精确的保留敏感性来减少噪声。

Method: 定义保留敏感性作为在固定保留数据集R情况下，删除集U引起的最坏情况输出变化。基于此概念重新分析两种广泛使用的认证遗忘算法，利用R引入的规律性进一步减少噪声。

Result: 理论分析和实验验证表明，保留敏感性方法能在多个问题（最小生成树权重、PCA、ERM）上显著减少噪声，同时提供相同的遗忘证书，提高算法效用。

Conclusion: 保留敏感性为认证机器遗忘提供了更精确的噪声校准框架，相比传统差分隐私方法能显著减少噪声添加，提高遗忘后模型的实用性，同时保持相同的统计不可区分性保证。

Abstract: Certified machine unlearning aims to provably remove the influence of a deletion set $U$ from a model trained on a dataset $S$, by producing an unlearned output that is statistically indistinguishable from retraining on the retain set $R:=S\setminus U$. Many existing certified unlearning methods adapt techniques from Differential Privacy (DP) and add noise calibrated to global sensitivity, i.e., the worst-case output change over all adjacent datasets. We show that this DP-style calibration is often overly conservative for unlearning, based on a key observation: certified unlearning, by definition, does not require protecting the privacy of the retained data $R$. Motivated by this distinction, we define retain sensitivity as the worst-case output change over deletions $U$ while keeping $R$ fixed. While insufficient for DP, retain sensitivity is exactly sufficient for unlearning, allowing for the same certificates with less noise. We validate these reductions in noise theoretically and empirically across several problems, including the weight of minimum spanning trees, PCA, and ERM. Finally, we refine the analysis of two widely used certified unlearning algorithms through the lens of retain sensitivity, leveraging the regularity induced by $R$ to further reduce noise and improve utility.

</details>


### [163] [Understanding and Mitigating Dataset Corruption in LLM Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03206)
*Cullen Anderson,Narmeen Oozeer,Foad Namjoo,Remy Ogasawara,Amirali Abdullah,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对比引导方法在推理时调整LLM生成行为简单有效，但对其在噪声或对抗性数据污染下的鲁棒性研究不足。研究发现该方法对适度污染具有鲁棒性，但大量数据被恶意篡改时会产生不良副作用，采用鲁棒均值估计器可缓解这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比引导方法在AI安全应用中日益广泛，但对其在噪声或对抗性数据污染下的鲁棒性缺乏理解。研究旨在分析该方法在面对训练数据污染时的脆弱性，并探索相应的防护措施。

Method: 研究对比引导方法在训练数据被污染时的鲁棒性。首先观察不同污染程度下的效果，然后分析各种污染类型的几何特征，最后提出防护措施——将高维均值计算步骤替换为最近开发的鲁棒均值估计器。

Result: 1) 对比引导对适度数据污染具有相当鲁棒性；2) 当训练数据中有相当比例被恶意篡改时，会产生明显的不良副作用；3) 通过鲁棒均值估计器替换传统均值计算步骤，能够有效缓解恶意污染带来的大部分不良影响。

Conclusion: 对比引导方法在AI安全应用中需要关注其对抗性鲁棒性。虽然对适度污染具有鲁棒性，但恶意数据篡改会引发安全问题。采用鲁棒均值估计器是有效的防护措施，为实际部署提供了安全保障。

Abstract: Contrastive steering has been shown as a simple and effective method to adjust the generative behavior of LLMs at inference time. It uses examples of prompt responses with and without a trait to identify a direction in an intermediate activation layer, and then shifts activations in this 1-dimensional subspace. However, despite its growing use in AI safety applications, the robustness of contrastive steering to noisy or adversarial data corruption is poorly understood. We initiate a study of the robustness of this process with respect to corruption of the dataset of examples used to train the steering direction. Our first observation is that contrastive steering is quite robust to a moderate amount of corruption, but unwanted side effects can be clearly and maliciously manifested when a non-trivial fraction of the training data is altered. Second, we analyze the geometry of various types of corruption, and identify some safeguards. Notably, a key step in learning the steering direction involves high-dimensional mean computation, and we show that replacing this step with a recently developed robust mean estimator often mitigates most of the unwanted effects of malicious corruption.

</details>


### [164] [I-CAM-UV: Integrating Causal Graphs over Non-Identical Variable Sets Using Causal Additive Models with Unobserved Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03207)
*Hirofumi Suzuki,Kentaro Kanamori,Takuya Takagi,Thong Pham,Takashi Nicholas Maeda,Shohei Shimizu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出I-CAM-UV方法，通过整合多个具有不同变量集合的数据集中的因果信息，处理未观测变量问题，构建更完整的因果图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中常遇到多个数据集具有非相同变量集合的情况，现有方法通常针对单个数据集设计。简单重叠各数据集因果图的方法受限，因为未观测变量可能成为混杂因子，且某些变量对在任何数据集中都未观测到。

Method: 利用CAM-UV（含未观测变量的因果可加模型）提供包含未观测变量信息的因果图。证明真实因果图与各数据集CAM-UV信息具有结构一致性。提出I-CAM-UV方法，通过枚举所有一致因果图来整合CAM-UV结果，并提供高效组合搜索算法。

Result: I-CAM-UV相比现有方法表现出更好的性能，能够更有效地整合多个数据集中的因果信息，处理未观测变量问题。

Conclusion: I-CAM-UV为解决多数据集因果发现问题提供了有效方法，特别适用于处理具有不同变量集合和未观测变量的复杂场景。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data is a fundamental tool in various fields of science. While existing approaches are typically designed for a single dataset, we often need to handle multiple datasets with non-identical variable sets in practice. One straightforward approach is to estimate a causal graph from each dataset and construct a single causal graph by overlapping. However, this approach identifies limited causal relationships because unobserved variables in each dataset can be confounders, and some variable pairs may be unobserved in any dataset. To address this issue, we leverage Causal Additive Models with Unobserved Variables (CAM-UV) that provide causal graphs having information related to unobserved variables. We show that the ground truth causal graph has structural consistency with the information of CAM-UV on each dataset. As a result, we propose an approach named I-CAM-UV to integrate CAM-UV results by enumerating all consistent causal graphs. We also provide an efficient combinatorial search algorithm and demonstrate the usefulness of I-CAM-UV against existing methods.

</details>


### [165] [Adaptive Methods Are Preferable in High Privacy Settings: An SDE Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03226)
*Enea Monzio Compagnoni,Alessandro Stanghellini,Rustem Islamov,Aurelien Lucchi,Anastasiia Koloskova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过随机微分方程分析DP优化器，发现DP-SGD和DP-SignSGD在固定超参数下隐私-效用权衡存在显著差异，但最优学习率下性能相当；自适应方法（如DP-SignSGD）的超参数在不同隐私级别间可迁移，更具实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着隐私法规趋严，差分隐私在大规模训练中变得至关重要。作者旨在通过随机微分方程重新审视DP噪声与优化自适应性的相互作用，为私有优化器提供首个基于SDE的分析框架。

Method: 采用随机微分方程理论分析DP优化器，重点关注DP-SGD和DP-SignSGD在逐样本裁剪下的行为。对比固定超参数和最优学习率两种设置下的收敛特性，并进行实证验证。

Result: 固定超参数下：DP-SGD以O(1/ε²)的隐私-效用权衡收敛，速度与ε无关；DP-SignSGD以线性于ε的速度收敛，具有O(1/ε)权衡，在高隐私或大批量噪声场景中占优。最优学习率下：两者理论渐近性能相当，但DP-SGD最优学习率与ε线性相关，而DP-SignSGD基本与ε无关，使自适应方法更具实用性。

Conclusion: 自适应DP优化器（如DP-SignSGD）的超参数在不同隐私级别间具有良好迁移性，无需重新调优，这使其在实际应用中比DP-SGD更具优势。实证结果支持理论分析，并可从DP-SignSGD扩展到DP-Adam。

Abstract: Differential Privacy (DP) is becoming central to large-scale training as privacy regulations tighten. We revisit how DP noise interacts with adaptivity in optimization through the lens of stochastic differential equations, providing the first SDE-based analysis of private optimizers. Focusing on DP-SGD and DP-SignSGD under per-example clipping, we show a sharp contrast under fixed hyperparameters: DP-SGD converges at a Privacy-Utility Trade-Off of $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon^2)$ with speed independent of $\varepsilon$, while DP-SignSGD converges at a speed linear in $\varepsilon$ with an $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon)$ trade-off, dominating in high-privacy or large batch noise regimes. By contrast, under optimal learning rates, both methods achieve comparable theoretical asymptotic performance; however, the optimal learning rate of DP-SGD scales linearly with $\varepsilon$, while that of DP-SignSGD is essentially $\varepsilon$-independent. This makes adaptive methods far more practical, as their hyperparameters transfer across privacy levels with little or no re-tuning. Empirical results confirm our theory across training and test metrics, and empirically extend from DP-SignSGD to DP-Adam.

</details>


### [166] [Coalgebras for categorical deep learning: Representability and universal approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03227)
*Dragan Mašulović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为深度学习的等变表示建立了余代数基础，将经典的群作用和等变映射推广到范畴论框架，并证明了在此框架下的通用逼近定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范畴深度学习（CDL）旨在为不同神经架构提供统一的范畴论框架，而几何深度学习（GDL）主要关注群作用下的不变性。本文旨在通过余代数形式为深度学习中的等变表示建立更一般化的理论基础，连接抽象不变性规范与具体神经架构实现。

Method: 采用余代数形式将经典的群作用和等变映射概念一般化。首先证明：给定从SET到VECT的数据集嵌入函子，以及在数据集上建模不变行为的SET自函子，存在相应的VECT自函子与嵌入兼容，能够恢复嵌入数据上的类似不变行为。在此基础上，建立广义设置下等变映射的通用逼近定理。

Result: 主要结果包括：1）建立了数据集嵌入与不变行为函子之间的兼容性提升；2）证明了在余代数框架下，对于广泛对称性类，连续等变函数可以被逼近的通用逼近定理。这为抽象不变性规范与神经架构具体实现之间建立了范畴桥梁。

Conclusion: 本文通过余代数形式为深度学习中的等变表示提供了范畴理论基础，将经典群作用概念推广到更一般的范畴论框架，并证明了在此框架下的通用逼近能力，为连接抽象不变性规范与具体神经架构实现提供了数学基础。

Abstract: Categorical deep learning (CDL) has recently emerged as a framework that leverages category theory to unify diverse neural architectures. While geometric deep learning (GDL) is grounded in the specific context of invariants of group actions, CDL aims to provide domain-independent abstractions for reasoning about models and their properties. In this paper, we contribute to this program by developing a coalgebraic foundation for equivariant representation in deep learning, as classical notions of group actions and equivariant maps are naturally generalized by the coalgebraic formalism. Our first main result demonstrates that, given an embedding of data sets formalized as a functor from SET to VECT, and given a notion of invariant behavior on data sets modeled by an endofunctor on SET, there is a corresponding endofunctor on VECT that is compatible with the embedding in the sense that this lifted functor recovers the analogous notion of invariant behavior on the embedded data. Building on this foundation, we then establish a universal approximation theorem for equivariant maps in this generalized setting. We show that continuous equivariant functions can be approximated within our coalgebraic framework for a broad class of symmetries. This work thus provides a categorical bridge between the abstract specification of invariant behavior and its concrete realization in neural architectures.

</details>


### [167] [Inverse Reconstruction of Shock Time Series from Shock Response Spectrum Curves using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03229)
*Adam Watts,Andrew Jeon,Destry Newton,Ryan Bowering*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）从目标冲击响应谱（SRS）直接生成加速度时程信号，避免传统迭代优化的计算负担


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SRS逆重构方法基于迭代优化和预定义基函数（如指数衰减正弦波），计算成本高且受限于基函数选择。从加速度时程到SRS的映射是非线性和多对一的，导致逆重构问题本质上是病态的

Method: 提出条件变分自编码器（CVAE）模型，学习从SRS到加速度时程的数据驱动逆映射。模型训练后可直接从目标谱生成符合要求的时程信号，无需迭代优化

Result: 相比传统方法，模型在谱保真度上有改进，对未见谱有强泛化能力，推理速度比传统方法快3-6个数量级

Conclusion: 深度生成建模为SRS逆重构提供了一种可扩展且高效的方法，解决了传统迭代优化方法的计算瓶颈

Abstract: The shock response spectrum (SRS) is widely used to characterize the response of single-degree-of-freedom (SDOF) systems to transient accelerations. Because the mapping from acceleration time history to SRS is nonlinear and many-to-one, reconstructing time-domain signals from a target spectrum is inherently ill-posed. Conventional approaches address this problem through iterative optimization, typically representing signals as sums of exponentially decayed sinusoids, but these methods are computationally expensive and constrained by predefined basis functions.
  We propose a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) that learns a data-driven inverse mapping from SRS to acceleration time series. Once trained, the model generates signals consistent with prescribed target spectra without requiring iterative optimization. Experiments demonstrate improved spectral fidelity relative to classical techniques, strong generalization to unseen spectra, and inference speeds three to six orders of magnitude faster. These results establish deep generative modeling as a scalable and efficient approach for inverse SRS reconstruction.

</details>


### [168] [SynthCharge: An Electric Vehicle Routing Instance Generator with Feasibility Screening to Enable Learning-Based Optimization and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03230)
*Mertcan Daysalilar,Fuat Uyguroglu,Gabriel Nicolosi,Adam Meyers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SynthCharge是一个参数化生成器，用于生成经过可行性筛选的电动汽车路径规划问题（EVRPTW）实例，支持不同时空配置和可扩展的客户规模，为学习型路由模型提供动态基准测试基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EVRPTW基准数据集通常是静态的，缺乏可验证的可行性，限制了学习型路由模型的可重复评估。需要动态、多样化的实例生成器来系统评估神经路由和数据驱动方法的鲁棒性。

Method: 开发SynthCharge参数化生成器，集成实例几何结构、自适应能量容量缩放和范围感知充电站布局。通过快速可行性筛选过程系统过滤不可解实例，保证结构有效性。可生成最多500个客户的大规模实例，实验中关注5-100个客户的规模。

Result: SynthCharge能够生成多样化、经过可行性筛选的EVRPTW实例，覆盖不同时空配置和客户规模。与静态基准套件不同，它提供了动态基准测试基础设施，支持系统评估新兴神经路由和数据驱动方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SynthCharge解决了现有EVRPTW基准数据集的局限性，通过参数化生成和可行性筛选机制，为学习型路由模型提供了可重复、可扩展的动态评估框架，促进了该领域研究的发展。

Abstract: The electric vehicle routing problem with time windows (EVRPTW) extends the classical VRPTW by introducing battery capacity constraints and charging station decisions. Existing benchmark datasets are often static and lack verifiable feasibility, which restricts reproducible evaluation of learning-based routing models. We introduce SynthCharge, a parametric generator that produces diverse, feasibility-screened EVRPTW instances across varying spatiotemporal configurations and scalable customer counts. While SynthCharge can currently generate large-scale instances of up to 500 customers, we focus our experiments on sizes ranging from 5 to 100 customers. Unlike static benchmark suites, SynthCharge integrates instance geometry with adaptive energy capacity scaling and range-aware charging station placement. To guarantee structural validity, the generator systematically filters out unsolvable instances through a fast feasibility screening process. Ultimately, SynthCharge provides the dynamic benchmarking infrastructure needed to systematically evaluate the robustness of emerging neural routing and data-driven approaches.

</details>


### [169] [ALARM: Automated MLLM-Based Anomaly Detection in Complex-EnviRonment Monitoring with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03101)
*Congjing Zhang,Feng Lin,Xinyi Zhao,Pei Guo,Wei Li,Lin Chen,Chaoyue Zhao,Shuai Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ALARM是一个集成不确定性量化(UQ)的多模态大语言模型视觉异常检测框架，通过推理链、自我反思和模型集成等技术提升复杂环境下的检测可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂环境中，视觉异常检测面临高度上下文相关性和模糊性的挑战，需要不确定性量化能力来确保多模态大语言模型检测系统的成功部署

Method: ALARM框架集成了不确定性量化与质量保证技术，包括推理链、自我反思和多模态大语言模型集成，基于严格的概率推理流程和计算过程设计

Result: 在真实世界智能家居基准数据和伤口图像分类数据上的广泛实证评估显示，ALARM具有优越性能，并在不同领域具有通用适用性，支持可靠决策

Conclusion: ALARM框架通过集成不确定性量化与质量保证技术，为复杂环境下的多模态大语言模型视觉异常检测提供了可靠解决方案，具有跨领域通用性

Abstract: The advance of Large Language Models (LLMs) has greatly stimulated research interest in developing multi-modal LLM (MLLM)-based visual anomaly detection (VAD) algorithms that can be deployed in complex environments. The challenge is that in these complex environments, the anomalies are sometimes highly contextual and also ambiguous, and thereby, uncertainty quantification (UQ) is a crucial capacity for an MLLM-based VAD system to succeed. In this paper, we introduce our UQ-supported MLLM-based VAD framework called ALARM. ALARM integrates UQ with quality-assurance techniques like reasoning chain, self-reflection, and MLLM ensemble for robust and accurate performance and is designed based on a rigorous probabilistic inference pipeline and computational process. Extensive empirical evaluations are conducted using the real-world smart-home benchmark data and wound image classification data, which shows ALARM's superior performance and its generic applicability across different domains for reliable decision-making.

</details>


### [170] [On Geometry Regularization in Autoencoder Reduced-Order Models with Latent Neural ODE Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03238)
*Mikhail Osipov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究几何正则化策略对编码器-解码器降阶模型中学习到的潜在表示的影响，发现Stiefel投影方法在提升潜在动力学条件数方面表现最佳


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究几何正则化策略如何影响编码器-解码器降阶模型中学习到的潜在表示，特别是在对流-扩散-反应方程固定实验设置下，评估不同正则化方法对后续潜在动力学训练的影响

Method: 在自编码器预训练阶段应用四种几何正则化方法：(a)解码器雅可比矩阵的近等距正则化，(b)基于随机方向增益的随机解码器增益惩罚，(c)二阶方向曲率惩罚，(d)第一解码器层的Stiefel投影；使用神经ODE建模潜在动力学，并在多个随机种子下评估

Result: 方法(a)-(c)通常使后续冻结自编码器的潜在动力学训练更加困难，特别是在长时程推演中，即使它们改善了局部解码器平滑度；方法(d)则持续改善学习到的潜在动力学的条件数相关诊断指标，并倾向于获得更好的推演性能

Conclusion: 在该设置中，潜在几何不匹配对下游任务的影响超过了改善解码器平滑度带来的益处；Stiefel投影方法在提升潜在动力学条件数方面表现最佳，表明几何正则化策略的选择需要仔细考虑其对后续动力学建模的影响

Abstract: We investigate geometric regularization strategies for learned latent representations in encoder--decoder reduced-order models. In a fixed experimental setting for the advection--diffusion--reaction (ADR) equation, we model latent dynamics using a neural ODE and evaluate four regularization approaches applied during autoencoder pre-training: (a) near-isometry regularization of the decoder Jacobian, (b) a stochastic decoder gain penalty based on random directional gains, (c) a second-order directional curvature penalty, and (d) Stiefel projection of the first decoder layer. Across multiple seeds, we find that (a)--(c) often produce latent representations that make subsequent latent-dynamics training with a frozen autoencoder more difficult, especially for long-horizon rollouts, even when they improve local decoder smoothness or related sensitivity proxies. In contrast, (d) consistently improves conditioning-related diagnostics of the learned latent dynamics and tends to yield better rollout performance. We discuss the hypothesis that, in this setting, the downstream impact of latent-geometry mismatch outweighs the benefits of improved decoder smoothness.

</details>


### [171] [Speculative Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03251)
*Tanishq Kumar,Tri Dao,Avner May*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SSD（推测性推测解码）算法，通过并行化推测和验证操作，在验证进行时预先生成多个推测结果，消除传统推测解码中的顺序依赖瓶颈，实现更快的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推测解码虽然通过快速草稿模型预测后续token并用目标模型并行验证来加速推理，但其推测和验证操作之间存在顺序依赖关系，这仍然是性能瓶颈。需要进一步并行化这些操作以获得更大的加速效果。

Method: 提出SSD（推测性推测解码）算法：在验证进行时，草稿模型预测可能的验证结果，并预先为这些结果准备相应的推测。如果实际验证结果在预测集合中，则可以立即返回推测，完全消除草稿生成的开销。针对SSD的三个关键挑战提出了原则性解决方法，并实现了优化的Saguaro算法。

Result: Saguaro实现比优化的推测解码基线快2倍，比使用开源推理引擎的自回归解码快5倍。SSD通过并行化推测和验证操作，显著减少了推理延迟。

Conclusion: SSD通过并行化推测和验证操作，解决了传统推测解码中的顺序依赖瓶颈。Saguaro算法实现了显著的推理加速，为大型语言模型的高效推理提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding is bottlenecked by its sequential nature. Speculative decoding has become a standard way to accelerate inference by using a fast draft model to predict upcoming tokens from a slower target model, and then verifying them in parallel with a single target model forward pass. However, speculative decoding itself relies on a sequential dependence between speculation and verification. We introduce speculative speculative decoding (SSD) to parallelize these operations. While a verification is ongoing, the draft model predicts likely verification outcomes and prepares speculations pre-emptively for them. If the actual verification outcome is then in the predicted set, a speculation can be returned immediately, eliminating drafting overhead entirely. We identify three key challenges presented by speculative speculative decoding, and suggest principled methods to solve each. The result is Saguaro, an optimized SSD algorithm. Our implementation is up to 2x faster than optimized speculative decoding baselines and up to 5x faster than autoregressive decoding with open source inference engines.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [A Novel Modular Cable-Driven Soft Robotic Arm with Multi-Segment Reconfigurability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02468)
*Moeen Ul Islam,Cheng Ouyang,Xinda Qi,Azlan Zahid,Xiaobo Tan,Dong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种模块化、缆绳驱动的软体机器人手臂，具有多段可重构性，通过堆叠模块实现工作空间扩展，并研究硅胶刚度对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发可重构的软体机器人系统，以适应不同的结构和应用需求，同时探索材料刚度对软体机器人性能的影响，平衡柔顺性与结构强度。

Method: 采用模块化、缆绳驱动的软体机器人手臂设计，使用软硅胶材料制造，嵌入肌腱导向通道和双螺旋保护结构，实现独立段控制，通过实验测试不同刚度硅胶的性能。

Result: 三段配置相比单段实现了平面工作空间面积13倍增加和体积38.9倍增加；较软硅胶提高弯曲灵活性，较硬硅胶增强结构刚度和负载稳定性，存在刚度与柔顺性的权衡。

Conclusion: 模块化堆叠显著扩展软体机器人工作空间，通过调整硅胶刚度可以平衡柔顺性与结构强度，为可重构软体机器人手臂的配置提供了有效方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel, modular, cable-driven soft robotic arm featuring multi-segment reconfigurability. The proposed architecture enables a stackable system with independent segment control, allowing scalable adaptation to diverse structural and application requirements. The system is fabricated from soft silicone material and incorporates embedded tendon-routing channels with a protective dual-helical tendon structure. Experimental results showed that modular stacking substantially expanded the reachable workspace: relative to the single-segment arm, the three-segment configuration achieved up to a 13-fold increase in planar workspace area and a 38.9-fold increase in workspace volume. Furthermore, this study investigated the effect of silicone stiffness on actuator performance. The results revealed a clear trade-off between compliance and stiffness: softer silicone improved bending flexibility, while stiffer silicone improved structural rigidity and load-bearing stability. These results highlight the potential of stiffness tuning to balance compliance and strength for configuring scalable, reconfigurable soft robotic arms.

</details>


### [173] [COLREGs Compliant Collision Avoidance and Grounding Prevention for Autonomous Marine Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02484)
*Mayur S. Patil,Nataraj Sudharsan,Veneela Ammula,Jude Tomdio,Jin Wang,Michael Kei,Sivakumar Rathinam,Prabhakar R. Pagilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于海上自主水面船舶的统一运动规划方法，实现实时避碰、遵守国际海上避碰规则和防搁浅


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上自主水面船舶在现实环境中的可靠部署面临挑战，特别是在拥挤水域，需要安全且符合法规的运动规划策略

Method: 采用凸优化方法，整合速度障碍碰撞约束、COLREGs方向约束和测深防搁浅约束，通过整数线性规划近似非凸浅水区域

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法在多船相遇场景中能产生安全且符合法规的机动，适合实时自主海上导航

Conclusion: 提出的统一运动规划方法能有效解决海上自主船舶的避碰、法规遵守和防搁浅问题，具有实时应用潜力

Abstract: Maritime Autonomous Surface Ships (MASS) are increasingly regarded as a promising solution to address crew shortages, improve navigational safety, and improve operational efficiency in the maritime industry. Nevertheless, the reliable deployment of MASS in real-world environments remains a significant challenge, particularly in congested waters where the majority of maritime accidents occur. This emphasizes the need for safe and regulation-aware motion planning strategies for MASS that are capable of operating under dynamic maritime conditions. This paper presents a unified motion planning method for MASS that achieves real time collision avoidance, compliance with International Regulations for Preventing Collisions at Sea (COLREGs), and grounding prevention. The proposed work introduces a convex optimization method that integrates velocity obstacle-based (VO) collision constraints, COLREGs-based directional constraints, and bathymetry-based grounding constraints to generate computationally efficient, rule-compliant optimal velocity selection. To enhance robustness, the classical VO method is extended to consider uncertainty in the position and velocity estimates of the target vessel. Unnavigable shallow water regions obtained from bathymetric data, which are inherently nonconvex, are approximated via convex geometries using a integer linear programming (ILP), allowing grounding constraints to be incorporated into the motion planning. The resulting optimization generates optimal and dynamically feasible input velocities that meet collision avoidance, regulatory compliance, kinodynamic limits, and grounding prevention requirements. Simulation results involving multi-vessel encounters demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in producing safe and regulation-compliant maneuvers, highlighting the suitability of the proposed approach for real time autonomous maritime navigation.

</details>


### [174] [A Robust Simulation Framework for Verification and Validation of Autonomous Maritime Navigation in Adverse Weather and Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02487)
*Mayur S. Patil,Nataraj Sudharsan,Anthony S. Saaiby,JiaChang Xing,Keliang Pan,Veneela Ammula,Jude Tomdio,Jin Wang,Michael Kei,Heonyong Kang,Sivakumar Rathinam,Prabhakar R. Pagilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个增强的虚拟仿真框架，用于在真实海洋环境中验证和确认海上自主水面船舶，特别关注天气和海底地形对自主导航性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上自主水面船舶(MASS)的可靠部署需要在各种环境条件下进行严格的验证和确认，包括极端和安全关键场景，但真实世界试验中难以或不安全地复现这些场景。

Method: 开发一个高保真环境建模套件，能够模拟恶劣天气条件（雨、雾、波浪动力学），并集成来自美国主要港口的高分辨率海底地形数据，实现深度感知导航和搁浅预防。

Result: 该框架提供了广泛的可配置性，能够在广泛的海洋条件下进行系统测试，包括在实际试验中不切实际或不安全的场景，从而支持MASS的验证和确认。

Conclusion: 所提出的增强虚拟仿真框架为MASS在真实海洋环境中的验证和确认提供了有效工具，特别关注天气和海底地形对自主导航性能的影响，有助于提高海上自主船舶的安全性和可靠性。

Abstract: Maritime Autonomous Surface Ships (MASS) have emerged as a promising solution to enhance navigational safety, operational efficiency, and long-term cost effectiveness. However, their reliable deployment requires rigorous verification and validation (V\&V) under various environmental conditions, including extreme and safety-critical scenarios. This paper presents an enhanced virtual simulation framework to support the V\&V of MASS in realistic maritime environments, with particular emphasis on the influence of weather and bathymetry on autonomous navigation performance. The framework incorporates a high-fidelity environmental modeling suite capable of simulating adverse weather conditions such as rain, fog, and wave dynamics. The key factors that affect weather, such as rain and visibility, are parameterized to affect sea-state characteristics, perception, and sensing systems, resulting in position and velocity uncertainty, reduced visibility, and degraded situational awareness. Furthermore, high-resolution bathymetric data from major U.S. ports are integrated to enable depth-aware navigation, grounding prevention capabilities, and evaluation of vessel controllability in shallow or confined waterways. The proposed framework offers extensive configurability, enabling systematic testing in a wide spectrum of maritime conditions, including scenarios that are impractical or unsafe to replicate in real-world trials, thus supporting the V\&V of MASS.

</details>


### [175] [Instant and Reversible Adhesive-free Bonding Between Silicones and Glossy Papers for Soft Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02500)
*Takumi Shibuya,Kazuya Murakami,Akitsu Shigetou,Jun Shintake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无粘合剂、溶剂可逆的硅胶-光面纸快速粘接方法，用于制造流体驱动软体执行器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统硅胶与非延展材料的集成方法依赖不可逆粘合剂或嵌入工艺，这些方法劳动密集且难以修改，需要更简单、可逆的制造方法

Method: 开发了硅胶-光面纸粘接方法，通过简单接触形成强界面，无需粘合剂，可通过乙醇浸泡完全分离和重新组装

Result: SGB界面剪切强度>61 kPa，可重复使用且性能不损失；软体执行器性能与传统嵌入层设计相当或更优，可实现收缩、弯曲和扭转等多种驱动模式

Conclusion: SGB方法简化了软体机器人制造过程，支持组件重用和快速迭代，为快速原型设计提供了可扩展且可持续的平台

Abstract: Integrating silicone with non-extensible materials is a common strategy used in the fabrication of fluidically-driven soft actuators, yet conventional approaches often rely on irreversible adhesives or embedding processes that are labor-intensive and difficult to modify. This work presents silicone-glossy paper bonding (SGB), a rapid, adhesive-free, and solvent-reversible bonding approach that forms robust silicone-paper interfaces simply through contact. The SGB interface withstands high mechanical loads (shear strength > 61 kPa) and can be fully detached and reassembled via ethanol immersion without loss of performance, enabling component reuse and rapid redesign. Characterization studies indicate that surface functional groups primarily govern adhesion on the glossy paper and the modulus of the silicone, while durability and environmental response clarify the conditions for reversible debonding. The results further suggest a synergistic interaction of hydrogen bonding and oligomer diffusion, yielding strong yet reconfigurable adhesion. Soft actuators fabricated using SGB design exhibit equal or greater performance compared to conventional embedded-layer design and enable programmable actuation modes, including contraction, bending, and twisting. By simplifying fabrication while supporting reuse and rapid iteration, SGB offers a scalable and sustainable platform for rapid prototyping in soft robotics.

</details>


### [176] [Learning Object-Centric Spatial Reasoning for Sequential Manipulation in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02511)
*Chrisantus Eze,Ryan C Julian,Christopher Crick*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Unveiler框架通过解耦空间推理与动作执行，专门解决密集杂乱环境中的机器人抓取问题，相比端到端模型具有更高的数据效率和模块化优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模端到端模型在密集杂乱环境中的物体抓取任务中缺乏数据效率和模块化，需要专门化、解耦的系统来提升性能。

Method: 提出Unveiler框架，包含轻量级Transformer空间关系编码器(SRE)和旋转不变动作解码器。SRE通过两阶段训练：启发式演示的模仿学习初始化，PPO微调发现超越启发式的移除策略。

Result: 在模拟环境中，部分遮挡场景成功率97.6%，完全遮挡场景成功率90.0%，超越经典端到端策略和现代大模型基线。SRE的空间推理能力可零样本迁移到真实场景。

Conclusion: 专门化、对象中心的空间推理在复杂操作任务中具有强大优势，解耦架构在计算效率、参数数量和推理时间方面更优，且无需重新训练即可应用于物理机器人。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation in cluttered environments presents a critical challenge for automation. Recent large-scale, end-to-end models demonstrate impressive capabilities but often lack the data efficiency and modularity required for retrieving objects in dense clutter. In this work, we argue for a paradigm of specialized, decoupled systems and present Unveiler, a framework that explicitly separates high-level spatial reasoning from low-level action execution. Unveiler's core is a lightweight, transformer-based Spatial Relationship Encoder (SRE) that sequentially identifies the most critical obstacle for removal. This discrete decision is then passed to a rotation-invariant Action Decoder for execution. We demonstrate that this decoupled architecture is not only more computationally efficient in terms of parameter count and inference time, but also significantly outperforms both classic end-to-end policies and modern, large-model-based baselines in retrieving targets from dense clutter. The SRE is trained in two stages: imitation learning from heuristic demonstrations provides sample-efficient initialization, after which PPO fine-tuning enables the policy to discover removal strategies that surpass the heuristic in dense clutter. Our results, achieving up to 97.6\% success in partially occluded and 90.0\% in fully occluded scenarios in simulation, make a case for the power of specialized, object-centric reasoning in complex manipulation tasks. Additionally, we demonstrate that the SRE's spatial reasoning transfers zero-shot to real scenes, and validate the full system on a physical robot requiring only geometric workspace calibration; no learned components are retrained.

</details>


### [177] [PathSpace: Rapid continuous map approximation for efficient SLAM using B-Splines in constrained environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02538)
*Aduen Benjumea,Andrew Bradley,Alexander Rast,Matthias Rolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PathSpace框架，使用连续B样条紧凑表示环境，在自主赛车场景中验证其能以更少资源达到与传统地标方法相当的精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语义SLAM技术仍主要依赖密集几何环境表示，限制了基于上下文约束的应用能力，需要更紧凑高效的表示方法

Method: 提出PathSpace框架，使用连续B样条紧凑表示环境，同时维护连续概率密度函数进行概率推理，利用B样条在SLAM中的多重优势插值和拟合离散稀疏环境

Result: 在自主赛车场景中测试，利用预设赛道特性产生显著简化的表示，在可比精度水平下减少资源使用，最小化精度损失

Conclusion: PathSpace框架通过B样条表示实现了环境紧凑表示和概率推理，在资源受限系统中具有应用潜力，为语义SLAM提供了新的表示方法

Abstract: Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) plays a crucial role in enabling autonomous vehicles to navigate previously unknown environments.
  Semantic SLAM mostly extends visual SLAM, leveraging the higher density information available to reason about the environment in a more human-like manner. This allows for better decision making by exploiting prior structural knowledge of the environment, usually in the form of labels. Current semantic SLAM techniques still mostly rely on a dense geometric representation of the environment, limiting their ability to apply constraints based on context. We propose PathSpace, a novel semantic SLAM framework that uses continuous B-splines to represent the environment in a compact manner, while also maintaining and reasoning through the continuous probability density functions required for probabilistic reasoning. This system applies the multiple strengths of B-splines in the context of SLAM to interpolate and fit otherwise discrete sparse environments. We test this framework in the context of autonomous racing, where we exploit pre-specified track characteristics to produce significantly reduced representations at comparable levels of accuracy to traditional landmark based methods and demonstrate its potential in limiting the resources used by a system with minimal accuracy loss.

</details>


### [178] [Give me scissors: Collision-Free Dual-Arm Surgical Assistive Robot for Instrument Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02553)
*Xuejin Luo,Shiquan Sun,Runshi Zhang,Ruizhi Zhang,Junchen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于视觉语言模型的免碰撞双臂手术辅助机器人，用于手术器械递送，通过零样本轨迹生成和实时障碍感知实现动态环境下的安全操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人刷手护士依赖预定义路径递送器械，在动态环境中泛化性差且存在安全风险，需要更智能、安全的解决方案

Method: 使用视觉语言模型零样本生成抓取和递送轨迹；提出实时障碍最小距离感知方法；构建统一二次规划框架实现反应式避障和自碰撞预防

Result: 实验验证显示系统在手术器械递送任务中达到83.33%成功率，所有试验中保持平滑、无碰撞运动

Conclusion: 提出的机器人系统通过智能轨迹生成和实时避障框架，有效解决了动态手术环境中器械递送的安全性和泛化性问题

Abstract: During surgery, scrub nurses are required to frequently deliver surgical instruments to surgeons, which can lead to physical fatigue and decreased focus. Robotic scrub nurses provide a promising solution that can replace repetitive tasks and enhance efficiency. Existing research on robotic scrub nurses relies on predefined paths for instrument delivery, which limits their generalizability and poses safety risks in dynamic environments. To address these challenges, we present a collision-free dual-arm surgical assistive robot capable of performing instrument delivery. A vision-language model is utilized to automatically generate the robot's grasping and delivery trajectories in a zero-shot manner based on surgeons' instructions. A real-time obstacle minimum distance perception method is proposed and integrated into a unified quadratic programming framework. This framework ensures reactive obstacle avoidance and self-collision prevention during the dual-arm robot's autonomous movement in dynamic environments. Extensive experimental validations demonstrate that the proposed robotic system achieves an 83.33% success rate in surgical instrument delivery while maintaining smooth, collision-free movement throughout all trials. The project page and source code are available at https://give-me-scissors.github.io/.

</details>


### [179] [Wukong-Omni: Design, Modeling and Control of a Multi-mode Robot for Air, Land, and Underwater Exploration with All-in-One Propulsion Unit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02602)
*Yufan Liu,Rixi Yu,Junjie Li,Yishuai Zeng,Zhenting Wen,Cheng Li,Haifei Zhu,Shikang Lian,Wei Meng,Fumin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Wukong-Omni是一种新型多模式机器人，能够在陆地、空中和水下运行，采用统一推进系统，通过创新的机械设计实现电机复用和推力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在洪水灾害救援场景中，部分淹没的建筑物会阻止空中机器人进入较低楼层，限制了任务执行效果。需要一种能够在陆地、空中和水下跨域运行的机器人来应对这一挑战。

Method: 提出Wukong-Omni机器人，采用创新的机械设计实现统一推进系统，通过电机复用提高推力生成。通过仿真和水箱实验优化效率和峰值推力，开发三种操作域的动态模型，并提出统一的跨域控制框架。

Result: 实验结果显示，与直接安装方法相比，推进效率提高了100%，最大推力增加了150%。综合实验验证了稳定的运动性能和跨域平滑过渡能力，室外实验进一步证明了在实际环境中的鲁棒性和适应性。

Conclusion: Wukong-Omni机器人通过统一的推进系统和创新的机械设计，成功实现了在陆地、空中和水下的跨域运行，为洪水灾害救援等复杂环境应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In flood disaster rescue scenarios, partially submerged buildings prevent aerial robots from accessing lower levels, limiting mission effectiveness. To address this challenge, this paper presents Wukong-Omni, a novel multimode robot capable of operating across land, air, and underwater using a unified propulsion system. The system is enabled by an innovative mechanical design that allows motor reuse and improves thrust generation. Efficiency and peak thrust are enhanced through simulation and tank-based optimization. Experimental results show a 100 percent improvement in propulsion efficiency and a 150 percent increase in maximum thrust compared with direct installation methods. Dynamic models for the three operating domains are developed, and a unified cross-domain control framework is proposed. Comprehensive experiments validate stable locomotion and smooth transition across domains. Outdoor experiments further demonstrate robustness and adaptability in real-world environments.

</details>


### [180] [Uni-Skill: Building Self-Evolving Skill Repository for Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02623)
*Senwei Xie,Yuntian Zhang,Ruiping Wang,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uni-Skill：一个支持技能感知规划和自动技能演化的统一技能中心框架，通过SkillFolder从大规模机器人视频中构建层次化技能库，实现无需人工干预的自适应规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于基础模型的技能中心方法依赖固定的技能库，限制了在新任务中的适应性，需要手动干预来扩展技能。为了解决这一问题，需要开发能够自动演化技能库的框架，实现自适应规划。

Method: 提出Uni-Skill框架，包含两个核心组件：1）技能感知规划模块，在现有技能不足时请求新技能实现；2）SkillFolder技能库，从大规模非结构化机器人视频构建层次化技能分类法，提供语义监督和空间轨迹参考，支持少样本技能推断。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的综合实验验证了Uni-Skill相对于现有基于VLM的技能中心方法的先进性能，展示了其高级推理能力和在广泛新任务中的强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: Uni-Skill通过自动技能演化机制和结构化技能检索，将技能获取从低效的人工标注转变为高效的离线结构检索，实现了自适应规划和零样本泛化，为机器人技能学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: While skill-centric approaches leverage foundation models to enhance generalization in compositional tasks, they often rely on fixed skill libraries, limiting adaptability to new tasks without manual intervention. To address this, we propose Uni-Skill, a Unified Skill-centric framework that supports skill-aware planning and facilitates automatic skill evolution. Unlike prior methods that restrict planning to predefined skills, Uni-Skill requests for new skill implementations when existing ones are insufficient, ensuring adaptable planning with self-augmented skill library. To support automatic implementation of diverse skills requested by the planning module, we construct SkillFolder, a VerbNet-inspired repository derived from large-scale unstructured robotic videos. SkillFolder introduces a hierarchical skill taxonomy that captures diverse skill descriptions at multiple levels of abstraction. By populating this taxonomy with large-scale, automatically annotated demonstrations, Uni-Skill shifts the paradigm of skill acquisition from inefficient manual annotation to efficient offline structural retrieval. Retrieved examples provide semantic supervision over behavior patterns and fine-grained references for spatial trajectories, enabling few-shot skill inference without deployment-time demonstrations. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world settings verify the state-of-the-art performance of Uni-Skill over existing VLM-based skill-centric approaches, highlighting its advanced reasoning capabilities and strong zero-shot generalization across a wide range of novel tasks.

</details>


### [181] [cuNRTO: GPU-Accelerated Nonlinear Robust Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02642)
*Jiawei Wang,Arshiya Taj Abdul,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: cuNRTO：基于CUDA的非线性鲁棒轨迹优化框架，通过两种动态优化架构（NRTO-DR和NRTO-FullADMM）解决SOCP约束计算瓶颈，实现GPU加速，最高139.6倍速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒轨迹优化在处理不确定性时能保证约束满足，但通常产生计算昂贵的二阶锥规划（SOCP）约束。现有方法计算负担重，需要高效求解器来应对实时自主系统需求。

Method: 提出cuNRTO框架，包含两种架构：1) NRTO-DR：利用Douglas-Rachford分裂法求解SOCP内层子问题，通过并行SOCP投影和稀疏直接求解降低计算负担；2) NRTO-FullADMM：新型变体，利用问题结构通过交替方向乘子法（ADMM）提升可扩展性。使用自定义CUDA内核实现SOC投影步骤，cuBLAS GEMM链更新反馈增益。

Result: 在独轮车、四旋翼和Franka机械臂模型上进行仿真实验验证，cuNRTO框架实现最高139.6倍的速度提升，显著提高了鲁棒轨迹优化的计算效率。

Conclusion: cuNRTO框架通过GPU加速和优化算法创新，有效解决了鲁棒轨迹优化中的SOCP计算瓶颈，为自主系统在不确定性下的实时决策提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Robust trajectory optimization enables autonomous systems to operate safely under uncertainty by computing control policies that satisfy the constraints for all bounded disturbances. However, these problems often lead to large Second Order Conic Programming (SOCP) constraints, which are computationally expensive. In this work, we propose the CUDA Nonlinear Robust Trajectory Optimization (cuNRTO) framework by introducing two dynamic optimization architectures that have direct application to robust decision-making and are implemented on CUDA. The first architecture, NRTO-DR, leverages the Douglas-Rachford (DR) splitting method to solve the SOCP inner subproblems of NRTO, thereby significantly reducing the computational burden through parallel SOCP projections and sparse direct solves. The second architecture, NRTO-FullADMM, is a novel variant that further exploits the problem structure to improve scalability using the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM). Finally, we provide GPU implementation of the proposed methodologies using custom CUDA kernels for SOC projection steps and cuBLAS GEMM chains for feedback gain updates. We validate the performance of cuNRTO through simulated experiments on unicycle, quadcopter, and Franka manipulator models, demonstrating speedup up to 139.6$\times$.

</details>


### [182] [Compositional Visual Planning via Inference-Time Diffusion Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02646)
*Yixin Zhang,Yunhao Luo,Utkarsh Aashu Mishra,Woo Chul Shin,Yongxin Chen,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Tweedie估计边界一致性的训练无关组合扩散方法，用于长时域机器人规划，无需额外训练即可生成全局一致的规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型擅长短时域规划，但扩展到长时域任务面临计算限制和训练数据不足的挑战。现有组合方法在噪声数据空间中分解假设失效，导致全局规划不一致

Method: 将长时域规划建模为重叠视频块链式因子图推理，使用预训练短时域视频扩散模型提供局部先验。在推理时通过同步和异步消息传递在Tweedie估计上强制边界一致性

Result: 相比现有基线有显著改进，能有效泛化到原始训练数据中未见过的起点-目标组合，无需额外训练

Conclusion: 在Tweedie估计上强制边界一致性是实现稳定组合生成的关键，提出的训练无关框架能生成全局一致的长期规划

Abstract: Diffusion models excel at short-horizon robot planning, yet scaling them to long-horizon tasks remains challenging due to computational constraints and limited training data. Existing compositional approaches stitch together short segments by separately denoising each component and averaging overlapping regions. However, this suffers from instability as the factorization assumption breaks down in noisy data space, leading to inconsistent global plans. We propose that the key to stable compositional generation lies in enforcing boundary agreement on the estimated clean data (Tweedie estimates) rather than on noisy intermediate states. Our method formulates long-horizon planning as inference over a chain-structured factor graph of overlapping video chunks, where pretrained short-horizon video diffusion models provide local priors. At inference time, we enforce boundary agreement through a novel combination of synchronous and asynchronous message passing that operates on Tweedie estimates, producing globally consistent guidance without requiring additional training. Our training-free framework demonstrates significant improvements over existing baselines, effectively generalizing to unseen start-goal combinations that were not present in the original training data. Project website: https://comp-visual-planning.github.io/

</details>


### [183] [Watch Your Step: Learning Semantically-Guided Locomotion in Cluttered Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02657)
*Denan Liang,Yuan Zhu,Ruimeng Liu,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Shenghai Yuan,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SemLoco是一个强化学习框架，通过语义地图和两阶段RL方法，使腿式机器人能够在密集杂乱环境中精确避障，避免踩踏低矮物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在粗糙地形上表现出良好的移动性，但在杂乱环境中使用仍存在安全隐患。主要问题是无法避免踩踏低矮物体（如高价值小设备或地面电缆），这源于高层语义理解与底层控制的脱节，以及实际运行中高程地图的误差。

Method: SemLoco采用两阶段强化学习方法，结合软硬约束，执行像素级立足点安全推理，实现更精确的足部放置。同时集成语义地图分配可通行性成本，而非仅依赖几何数据。

Result: SemLoco显著减少了碰撞，提高了敏感物体周围的安全性，在传统控制器可能造成损坏的情况下实现了可靠导航。实验结果表明该方法能有效应用于更复杂的非结构化真实环境。

Conclusion: SemLoco通过结合语义理解和强化学习控制，解决了腿式机器人在密集杂乱环境中精确避障的关键问题，为安全导航提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Although legged robots demonstrate impressive mobility on rough terrain, using them safely in cluttered environments remains a challenge. A key issue is their inability to avoid stepping on low-lying objects, such as high-cost small devices or cables on flat ground. This limitation arises from a disconnection between high-level semantic understanding and low-level control, combined with errors in elevation maps during real-world operation. To address this, we introduce SemLoco, a Reinforcement Learning (RL) framework designed to avoid obstacles precisely in densely cluttered environments. SemLoco uses a two-stage RL approach that combines both soft and hard constraints and performs pixel-wise foothold safety inference, enabling more accurate foot placement. Additionally, SemLoco integrates a semantic map to assign traversability costs rather than relying solely on geometric data. SemLoco significantly reduces collisions and improves safety around sensitive objects, enabling reliable navigation in situations where traditional controllers would likely cause damage. Experimental results further demonstrate that SemLoco can be effectively applied to more complex, unstructured real-world environments.

</details>


### [184] [IMR-LLM: Industrial Multi-Robot Task Planning and Program Generation using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02669)
*Xiangyu Su,Juzhan Xu,Oliver van Kaick,Kai Xu,Ruizhen Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IMR-LLM框架，利用大语言模型进行工业多机器人任务规划与程序生成，通过构建析取图和确定性求解获得高效高层任务计划，再用过程树指导生成可执行低层程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中多机器人协作面临严格的顺序约束和复杂依赖关系，现有基于大语言模型的机器人协调方法难以应对这些挑战，需要专门针对工业环境设计新的规划框架。

Method: 提出IMR-LLM框架：1) 利用LLM辅助构建析取图，采用确定性求解方法获得可行高效的高层任务计划；2) 基于过程树指导LLM生成可执行的低层程序；3) 创建IMR-Bench基准测试集，包含三个复杂度级别的多机器人工业任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在所有评估指标上显著超越现有方法，验证了IMR-LLM在工业多机器人任务规划与程序生成方面的有效性。

Conclusion: IMR-LLM框架成功解决了工业场景中多机器人协作的复杂规划问题，通过结合LLM的推理能力和确定性求解方法，实现了高效的任务分解与程序生成，为工业自动化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In modern industrial production, multiple robots often collaborate to complete complex manufacturing tasks. Large language models (LLMs), with their strong reasoning capabilities, have shown potential in coordinating robots for simple household and manipulation tasks. However, in industrial scenarios, stricter sequential constraints and more complex dependencies within tasks present new challenges for LLMs. To address this, we propose IMR-LLM, a novel LLM-driven Industrial Multi-Robot task planning and program generation framework. Specifically, we utilize LLMs to assist in constructing disjunctive graphs and employ deterministic solving methods to obtain a feasible and efficient high-level task plan. Based on this, we use a process tree to guide LLMs to generate executable low-level programs. Additionally, we create IMR-Bench, a challenging benchmark that encompasses multi-robot industrial tasks across three levels of complexity. Experimental results indicate that our method significantly surpasses existing methods across all evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [185] [MMH-Planner: Multi-Mode Hybrid Trajectory Planning Method for UAV Efficient Flight Based on Real-Time Spatial Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02683)
*Yinghao Zhao,Chenguang Dai,Liang Lyu,Zhenchao Zhang,Chaozhen Lan,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于实时环境感知的无人机多模式混合轨迹规划方法，通过动态选择最优规划模型、目标导向空间感知和惰性重规划策略，显著提升规划效率和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前运动规划算法存在策略僵化和适应性弱的问题，导致规划效率受限，难以满足智能无人系统复杂自主操作的需求。

Method: 1. 目标导向空间感知方法快速评估飞行安全性；2. 多模式混合轨迹规划机制根据先验空间感知选择最优规划模型；3. 惰性重规划策略仅在必要时触发重规划以减少计算资源消耗。

Result: 仿真实验表明该方法在多项指标上优于现有SOTA算法，特别是在平均规划迭代次数和单次迭代计算成本方面表现最佳。真实飞行实验进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的多模式混合轨迹规划方法通过动态模型选择和智能重规划策略，显著提升了无人机轨迹规划的效率和适应性，为智能无人系统提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Motion planning is a critical component of intelligent unmanned systems, enabling their complex autonomous operations. However, current planning algorithms still face limitations in planning efficiency due to inflexible strategies and weak adaptability. To address this, this paper proposes a multi-mode hybrid trajectory planning method for UAVs based on real-time environmental awareness, which dynamically selects the optimal planning model for high-quality trajectory generation in response to environmental changes. First, we introduce a goal-oriented spatial awareness method that rapidly assesses flight safety in the upcoming environments. Second, a multi-mode hybrid trajectory planning mechanism is proposed, which can enhance the planning efficiency by selecting the optimal planning model for trajectory generation based on prior spatial awareness. Finally, we design a lazy replanning strategy that triggers replanning only when necessary to reduce computational resource consumption while maintaining flight quality. To validate the performance of the proposed method, we conducted comprehensive comparative experiments in simulation environments. Results demonstrate that our approach outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) algorithms across multiple metrics, achieving the best performance particularly in terms of the average number of planning iterations and computational cost per iteration. Furthermore, the effectiveness of our approach is further verified through real-world flight experiments integrated with a self-developed intelligent UAV platform.

</details>


### [186] [Robust Tightly-Coupled Filter-Based Monocular Visual-Inertial State Estimation and Graph-Based Evaluation for Autonomous Drone Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02742)
*Maulana Bisyir Azhari,Donghun Han,SungJun Park,David Hyunchul Shim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ADR-VINS框架，一种基于误差状态卡尔曼滤波的鲁棒单目视觉惯性状态估计系统，专为自主无人机竞速设计，通过直接像素重投影误差处理门角特征，最少只需两个可见角点，并在A2RL无人机锦标赛中成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自主无人机竞速的状态估计方法存在局限性：传统视觉惯性流程依赖松耦合的门基PnP校正，需要四个可见特征点且中间步骤存在信息损失；在无GNSS和运动捕捉系统的非仪器化竞速环境中，系统性能评估困难。

Method: 1. ADR-VINS：基于误差状态卡尔曼滤波的单目视觉惯性状态估计框架，将门角特征的直接像素重投影误差作为滤波器创新项，绕过中间PnP求解器，最少只需两个可见角点，使用鲁棒重加权而非RANSAC处理异常值。2. ADR-FGO：离线因子图优化框架，用于生成高保真参考轨迹，便于在无GNSS环境中进行飞行后性能评估。

Result: 在TII-RATM数据集上验证：ADR-VINS平均RMS平移误差为0.134米，ADR-FGO作为平滑参考达到0.060米。在A2RL无人机锦标赛第二季成功部署，在最高速度20.9米/秒的高机动飞行中，即使存在噪声检测也能保持稳定鲁棒的估计。

Conclusion: ADR-VINS为自主无人机竞速提供了计算高效且鲁棒的状态估计解决方案，能够处理极端速度和机动下的感知退化；ADR-FGO为无GNSS环境中的性能评估提供了有效工具，整套系统在实际竞速场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing (ADR) demands state estimation that is simultaneously computationally efficient and resilient to the perceptual degradation experienced during extreme velocity and maneuvers. Traditional frameworks typically rely on conventional visual-inertial pipelines with loosely-coupled gate-based Perspective-n-Points (PnP) corrections that suffer from a rigid requirement for four visible features and information loss in intermediate steps. Furthermore, the absence of GNSS and Motion Capture systems in uninstrumented, competitive racing environments makes the objective evaluation of such systems remarkably difficult. To address these limitations, we propose ADR-VINS, a robust, monocular visual-inertial state estimation framework based on an Error-State Kalman Filter (ESKF) tailored for autonomous drone racing. Our approach integrates direct pixel reprojection errors from gate corners features as innovation terms within the filter. By bypassing intermediate PnP solvers, ADR-VINS maintains valid state updates with as few as two visible corners and utilizes robust reweighting instead of RANSAC-based schemes to handle outliers, enhancing computational efficiency. Furthermore, we introduce ADR-FGO, an offline Factor-Graph Optimization framework to generate high-fidelity reference trajectories that facilitate post-flight performance evaluation and analysis on uninstrumented, GNSS-denied environments. The proposed system is validated using TII-RATM dataset, where ADR-VINS achieves an average RMS translation error of 0.134 m, while ADR-FGO yields 0.060 m as a smoothing-based reference. Finally, ADR-VINS was successfully deployed in the A2RL Drone Championship Season 2, maintaining stable and robust estimation despite noisy detections during high-agility flight at top speeds of 20.9 m/s. We further utilize ADR-FGO for post-flight evaluation in uninstrumented racing environments.

</details>


### [187] [Generative adversarial imitation learning for robot swarms: Learning from human demonstrations and trained policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02783)
*Mattes Kraus,Jonas Kuckling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于生成对抗模仿学习的群体机器人模仿学习框架，能够从人类演示中学习集体行为，并在真实机器人上验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群体机器人模仿学习大多基于已有策略的演示，本文旨在开发能够从人类演示中学习集体行为的框架

Method: 采用生成对抗模仿学习框架，从人类手动演示和PPO训练策略的演示中学习，在六个不同任务中评估

Result: 模仿学习能够学习到具有定性意义的行为，性能与提供的演示相当；在真实TurtleBot 4机器人上部署时，行为保持视觉可识别特征且性能与仿真相当

Conclusion: 提出的生成对抗模仿学习框架能够有效从人类演示中学习群体机器人集体行为，并在真实机器人上成功部署验证

Abstract: In imitation learning, robots are supposed to learn from demonstrations of the desired behavior. Most of the work in imitation learning for swarm robotics provides the demonstrations as rollouts of an existing policy. In this work, we provide a framework based on generative adversarial imitation learning that aims to learn collective behaviors from human demonstrations. Our framework is evaluated across six different missions, learning both from manual demonstrations and demonstrations derived from a PPO-trained policy. Results show that the imitation learning process is able to learn qualitatively meaningful behaviors that perform similarly well as the provided demonstrations. Additionally, we deploy the learned policies on a swarm of TurtleBot 4 robots in real-robot experiments. The exhibited behaviors preserved their visually recognizable character and their performance is comparable to the one achieved in simulation.

</details>


### [188] [SPARC: Spatial-Aware Path Planning via Attentive Robot Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02845)
*Sayang Mu,Xiangyu Wu,Bo An*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RMHA通信机制，通过显式嵌入曼哈顿距离来增强多机器人路径规划中的注意力权重计算，使机器人能动态优先处理空间相关邻居的消息，在拥挤区域显著提升协调效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习通信方法对所有相邻机器人一视同仁，忽略了空间邻近性，导致在协调至关重要的拥挤区域注意力被稀释，需要更有效的通信机制来提升多机器人路径规划效率。

Method: 提出关系增强多头注意力(RMHA)：1)在注意力权重计算中显式嵌入成对曼哈顿距离；2)使用距离约束注意力掩码；3)GRU门控消息融合；4)与MAPPO无缝集成进行稳定端到端训练。

Result: 在40x40网格上，从8个训练机器人零样本泛化到128个测试机器人：在30%障碍物密度下达到约75%成功率，比最佳基线高出25个百分点以上。消融研究证实距离关系编码是高密度环境下成功率提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: RMHA通过显式编码空间距离关系，使机器人能够动态优先处理相关邻居的消息，显著提升了多机器人路径规划在拥挤环境中的协调效率和成功率，验证了空间关系感知通信的重要性。

Abstract: Efficient communication is critical for decentralized Multi-Robot Path Planning (MRPP), yet existing learned communication methods treat all neighboring robots equally regardless of their spatial proximity, leading to diluted attention in congested regions where coordination matters most. We propose Relation enhanced Multi Head Attention (RMHA), a communication mechanism that explicitly embeds pairwise Manhattan distances into the attention weight computation, enabling each robot to dynamically prioritize messages from spatially relevant neighbors. Combined with a distance-constrained attention mask and GRU gated message fusion, RMHA integrates seamlessly with MAPPO for stable end-to-end training. In zero-shot generalization from 8 training robots to 128 test robots on 40x40 grids, RMHA achieves approximately 75 percent success rate at 30 percent obstacle density outperforming the best baseline by over 25 percentage points. Ablation studies confirm that distance-relation encoding is the key contributor to success rate improvement in high-density environments. Index Terms-Multi-robot path planning, graph attention mechanism, multi-head attention, communication optimization, cooperative decision-making

</details>


### [189] [Design, Modeling and Direction Control of a Wire-Driven Robotic Fish Based on a 2-DoF Crank-Slider Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02851)
*Yita Wang,Chen Chen,Yicheng Chen,Jinjie Li,Yuichi Motegi,Kenji Ohkuma,Toshihiro Maki,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种采用2自由度曲柄滑块机构的线驱动机器鱼，通过解耦推进与转向功能，同时实现了高速游泳和敏捷机动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BCF模式机器鱼存在推进与机动性的权衡：多舵机驱动方式游泳速度有限，而单电机线/杆驱动方式机动性差、难以平滑转向。需要解决这一折衷问题。

Method: 设计采用2自由度曲柄滑块机构的线驱动机器鱼，包括弹性骨架、防水结构和实现解耦的驱动机构；建立驱动建模和身体动力学分析运动行为；提出前馈-反馈组合控制策略独立调节推进和转向。

Result: 通过原型实验验证了设计、建模和控制的可行性，展示了游泳、转向和方向控制功能，实现了高速游泳和敏捷机动的双重目标。

Conclusion: 提出的2自由度曲柄滑块机构线驱动机器鱼成功解决了传统BCF机器鱼在推进速度与机动性之间的折衷问题，为环境监测和生物调查应用提供了更优的机器人平台。

Abstract: Robotic fish have attracted growing attention in recent years owing to their biomimetic design and potential applications in environmental monitoring and biological surveys. Among robotic fish employing the Body-Caudal Fin (BCF) locomotion pattern, motor-driven actuation is widely adopted. Some approaches utilize multiple servo motors to achieve precise body curvature control, while others employ a brushless motor to drive the tail via wire or rod, enabling higher oscillation and swimming speeds. However, the former approaches typically result in limited swimming speed, whereas the latter suffer from poor maneuverability, with few capable of smooth turning. To address this trade-off, we develop a wire-driven robotic fish equipped with a 2-degree-of-freedom (DoF) crank-slider mechanism that decouples propulsion from steering, enabling both high swimming speed and agile maneuvering. In this paper, we first present the design of the robotic fish, including the elastic skeleton, waterproof structure, and the actuation mechanism that realizes the decoupling. We then establish the actuation modeling and body dynamics to analyze the locomotion behavior. Furthermore, we propose a combined feedforward-feedback control strategy to achieve independent regulation of propulsion and steering. Finally, we validate the feasibility of the design, modeling, and control through a series of prototype experiments, demonstrating swimming, turning, and directional control.

</details>


### [190] [CoFL: Continuous Flow Fields for Language-Conditioned Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02854)
*Haokun Liu,Zhaoqi Ma,Yicheng Chen,Masaki Kitagawa,Wentao Zhang,Jinjie Li,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoFL是一种端到端导航策略，直接将鸟瞰图观测和语言指令映射到连续流场，通过数值积分生成平滑轨迹，在未见场景中显著优于模块化VLM规划器和生成策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决语言条件导航中模块化组件脆弱性和动作序列生成成本高的问题，开发更鲁棒、高效的端到端导航策略。

Method: 提出CoFL端到端策略，将鸟瞰图观测和语言指令直接映射到连续流场，通过数值积分生成轨迹；构建包含50万+BEV图像-指令对的数据集，每个样本通过程序化标注包含流场和轨迹。

Result: CoFL在严格未见场景中显著优于模块化VLM规划器和生成策略基线；在真实世界实验中实现零样本部署，保持可靠的闭环控制和高成功率。

Conclusion: CoFL通过连续流场表示和数值积分提供了一种有效的端到端导航方法，克服了传统方法的局限性，在模拟和真实环境中都表现出色。

Abstract: Language-conditioned navigation pipelines often rely on brittle modular components or costly action-sequence generation. To address these limitations, we present CoFL, an end-to-end policy that directly maps a bird's-eye view (BEV) observation and a language instruction to a continuous flow field for navigation. Instead of predicting discrete action tokens or sampling action chunks via iterative denoising, CoFL outputs instantaneous velocities that can be queried at arbitrary 2D projected locations. Trajectories are obtained by numerical integration of the predicted field, producing smooth motion that remains reactive under closed-loop execution. To enable large-scale training, we build a dataset of over 500k BEV image-instruction pairs, each procedurally annotated with a flow field and a trajectory derived from BEV semantic maps built on Matterport3D and ScanNet. By training on a mixed distribution, CoFL significantly outperforms modular Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based planners and generative policy baselines on strictly unseen scenes. Finally, we deploy CoFL zero-shot in real-world experiments with overhead BEV observations across multiple layouts, maintaining reliable closed-loop control and a high success rate.

</details>


### [191] [Rhythm: Learning Interactive Whole-Body Control for Dual Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02856)
*Hongjin Chen,Wei Zhang,Pengfei Li,Shihao Ma,Ke Ma,Yujie Jin,Zijun Xu,Xiaohui Wang,Yupeng Zheng,Zining Wang,Jieru Zhao,Yilun Chen,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Rhythm框架首次实现双人形机器人系统的真实世界部署，通过交互感知运动重定向、交互引导强化学习和部署系统，成功将拥抱、舞蹈等复杂交互行为从仿真迁移到现实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管单个人形机器人的敏捷性已有显著提升，但实现物理耦合的多个人形机器人交互仍面临巨大挑战，主要由于严重的运动学不匹配和复杂的接触动力学问题。现有方法难以实现真实世界中的多个人形机器人复杂交互控制。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：1）交互感知运动重定向模块，从人类数据生成可行的人形机器人交互参考；2）交互引导强化学习策略，通过基于图的奖励机制掌握耦合动力学；3）真实世界部署系统，实现双人形机器人交互的鲁棒迁移。

Result: 在物理Unitree G1机器人上的大量实验表明，该框架实现了鲁棒的交互全身控制，成功将拥抱、舞蹈等多种行为从仿真迁移到现实世界。

Conclusion: Rhythm是首个实现双人形机器人系统真实世界部署的统一框架，为多个人形机器人的复杂协作交互提供了可行的解决方案，推动了物理耦合多机器人系统的发展。

Abstract: Realizing interactive whole-body control for multi-humanoid systems is critical for unlocking complex collaborative capabilities in shared environments.
