Although recent advancements have significantly enhanced the agility of individual robots, bridging the gap to physically coupled multi-humanoid interaction remains challenging, primarily due to severe kinematic mismatches and complex contact dynamics. To address this, we introduce Rhythm, the first unified framework enabling real-world deployment of dual-humanoid systems for complex, physically plausible interactions. Our framework integrates three core components: (1) an Interaction-Aware Motion Retargeting (IAMR) module that generates feasible humanoid interaction references from human data; (2) an Interaction-Guided Reinforcement Learning (IGRL) policy that masters coupled dynamics via graph-based rewards; and (3) a real-world deployment system that enables robust transfer of dual-humanoid interaction. Extensive experiments on physical Unitree G1 robots demonstrate that our framework achieves robust interactive whole-body control, successfully transferring diverse behaviors such as hugging and dancing from simulation to reality.

</details>


### [192] [Emerging trends in Cislunar Space for Lunar Science Exploration and Space Robotics aiding Human Spaceflight Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02878)
*Arsalan Muhammad,Yue Wang,Hai Huang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文探讨人工智能和空间机器人技术在月球科学探索和载人航天任务中的关键作用，旨在加速人类星际探索进程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球作为独特的地外试验平台，对推动前沿科技发展、实现可持续人类存在和支持未来星际探索至关重要。研究旨在识别和探索具有变革潜力的关键领域，以加速人类星际探索目标。

Method: 研究识别并深入分析两个关键研究领域：1）人工智能与空间机器人技术在月球科学探索中的应用；2）空间机器人在载人登月任务中的支持作用，包括基础设施预部署、原位资源利用、表面操作支持和宇航员安全保障。

Result: 通过整合自主性、机器学习和实时传感器融合技术，空间机器人不仅增强了人类能力，还成为实现可持续月球探索的倍增器，为未来火星及更远载人任务铺平道路。

Conclusion: 人工智能和空间机器人技术是推动月球探索和未来星际任务的关键赋能技术，通过重新定义空间探索边界和提供关键任务支持，将加速人类实现可持续地外存在和星际探索的宏伟目标。

Abstract: In recent years, the Moon has emerged as an unparalleled extraterrestrial testbed for advancing cuttingedge technological and scientific research critical to enabling sustained human presence on its surface and supporting future interplanetary exploration. This study identifies and investigates two pivotal research domains with substantial transformative potential for accelerating humanity interplanetary aspirations. First is Lunar Science Exploration with Artificial Intelligence and Space Robotics which focusses on AI and Space Robotics redefining the frontiers of space exploration. Second being Space Robotics aid in manned spaceflight to the Moon serving as critical assets for pre-deployment infrastructure development, In-Situ Resource Utilization, surface operations support, and astronaut safety assurance. By integrating autonomy, machine learning, and realtime sensor fusion, space robotics not only augment human capabilities but also serve as force multipliers in achieving sustainable lunar exploration, paving the way for future crewed missions to Mars and beyond.

</details>


### [193] [Tracing Back Error Sources to Explain and Mitigate Pose Estimation Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02881)
*Loris Schneider,Yitian Shi,Rosa Wolf,Carolin Brenner,Rudolph Triebel,Rania Rayyes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出模块化、不确定性感知的位姿估计框架，通过故障检测、误差归因和针对性恢复，显著提升ICP的鲁棒性，在机器人抓取任务中实现与基础模型相当的性能但更简单快速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础通用位姿估计器试图在单一模型中处理各种误差源，但难以应对环境不确定性，且推理时间长、计算量大。需要更高效、鲁棒的解决方案

Method: 提出模块化、不确定性感知框架，将位姿估计分解为：故障检测、误差归因和针对性恢复。以ICP作为轻量级位姿估计器实例化该框架，仅必要时应用针对性缓解策略

Result: 显著提升了ICP的鲁棒性，在真实世界机器人抓取任务中实现了与基础模型竞争的性能，同时依赖更简单、更快速的位姿估计器

Conclusion: 模块化、不确定性感知的框架通过将位姿估计分解为故障检测、误差归因和针对性恢复，能够有效提升简单位姿估计器的鲁棒性，为机器人操作提供更高效、可靠的解决方案

Abstract: Robust estimation of object poses in robotic manipulation is often addressed using foundational general estimators, that aim to handle diverse error sources naively within a single model. Still, they struggle due to environmental uncertainties, while requiring long inference times and heavy computation. In contrast, we propose a modular, uncertainty-aware framework that attributes pose estimation errors to specific error sources and applies targeted mitigation strategies only when necessary. Instantiated with Iterative Closest Point (ICP) as a simple and lightweight pose estimator, we leverage our framework for real-world robotic grasping tasks. By decomposing pose estimation into failure detection, error attribution, and targeted recovery, we significantly improve the robustness of ICP and achieve competitive performance compared to foundation models, while relying on a substantially simpler and faster pose estimator.

</details>


### [194] [DreamFlow: Local Navigation Beyond Observation via Conditional Flow Matching in the Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02976)
*Jiwon Park,Dongkyu Lee,I Made Aswin Nahrendra,Jaeyoung Lim,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamFlow：基于深度强化学习的局部导航框架，通过条件流匹配扩展机器人感知范围，预测未观测环境特征以主动避免局部最小值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局部规划器在密集障碍物环境中易陷入局部最小值，而基于深度强化学习的方法受限于机载传感器有限的感知范围，导致导航失败。机器人无法感知视野外的环境结构是主要问题。

Method: 提出DreamFlow框架：1）使用条件流匹配学习局部高度图潜在表示与更广空间表示之间的概率映射；2）该预测模块以导航上下文为条件，使导航策略能够预测未观测环境特征；3）基于深度强化学习的导航策略利用预测信息主动避免潜在局部最小值。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）DreamFlow在潜在预测准确性方面优于现有方法；2）在仿真环境中导航性能表现更优；3）在四足机器人的真实杂乱环境中进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: DreamFlow通过条件流匹配扩展机器人感知范围，使导航策略能够预测未观测环境特征，有效解决了密集障碍物环境中的局部最小值问题，在仿真和真实环境中均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Local navigation in cluttered environments often suffers from dense obstacles and frequent local minima. Conventional local planners rely on heuristics and are prone to failure, while deep reinforcement learning(DRL)based approaches provide adaptability but are constrained by limited onboard sensing. These limitations lead to navigation failures because the robot cannot perceive structures outside its field of view. In this paper, we propose DreamFlow, a DRL-based local navigation framework that extends the robot's perceptual horizon through conditional flow matching(CFM). The proposed CFM based prediction module learns probabilistic mapping between local height map latent representation and broader spatial representation conditioned on navigation context. This enables the navigation policy to predict unobserved environmental features and proactively avoid potential local minima. Experimental results demonstrate that DreamFlow outperforms existing methods in terms of latent prediction accuracy and navigation performance in simulation. The proposed method was further validated in cluttered real world environments with a quadrupedal robot. The project page is available at https://dreamflow-icra.github.io.

</details>


### [195] [CASSR: Continuous A-Star Search through Reachability for real time footstep planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02989)
*Jiayi Wang,Steve Tonneau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CASSR框架通过递归传播凸连续运动学约束，结合EPA启发式，实现高效足步规划，比传统方法快100倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统A*方法需要离散化可达性约束，而混合整数规划(MIP)虽然支持连续公式但计算复杂度高，特别是包含旋转时。需要一种能处理连续约束且高效的方法用于足步规划。

Method: 提出CASSR框架，在A*搜索中递归传播机器人运动学约束的凸连续公式。结合基于EPA算法的新启发式函数，高效规划接触序列。

Result: CASSR能在125毫秒内规划多达30个足步，比传统离散化A*快100倍，且优于商业MIP求解器。在双足机器人运动任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: CASSR实现了快速、可靠、实时的双足机器人足步规划，为连续约束处理提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Footstep planning involves a challenging combinatorial search. Traditional A* approaches require discretising reachability constraints, while Mixed-Integer Programming (MIP) supports continuous formulations but quickly becomes intractable, especially when rotations are included. We present CASSR, a novel framework that recursively propagates convex, continuous formulations of a robot's kinematic constraints within an A* search. Combined with a new cost-to-go heuristic based on the EPA algorithm, CASSR efficiently plans contact sequences of up to 30 footsteps in under 125 ms. Experiments on biped locomotion tasks demonstrate that CASSR outperforms traditional discretised A* by up to a factor of 100, while also surpassing a commercial MIP solver. These results show that CASSR enables fast, reliable, and real-time footstep planning for biped robots.

</details>


### [196] [MA-CoNav: A Master-Slave Multi-Agent Framework with Hierarchical Collaboration and Dual-Level Reflection for Long-Horizon Embodied VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03024)
*Ling Luo,Qianqian Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MA-CoNav：基于分布式认知理论的多智能体协作导航框架，采用主从架构将导航任务分解到专门智能体，显著提升复杂长距离视觉语言导航性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法在复杂长距离任务中常因单个智能体认知过载导致感知失真和决策漂移，需要更高效的"语言理解-视觉感知-具身执行"闭环

Method: 提出多智能体协作导航框架MA-CoNav，采用主从分层架构：主智能体负责全局协调，从属智能体组分工协作（观察、规划、执行、记忆），并引入"局部-全局"双阶段反思机制动态优化导航流程

Result: 在Limo Pro机器人收集的真实室内数据集上实验，无需场景特定微调，MA-CoNav在多项指标上全面优于现有主流VLN方法

Conclusion: 分布式智能体协作架构能有效解决复杂长距离导航中的认知过载问题，为视觉语言导航提供了新的高效解决方案

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) aims to empower robots with the ability to perform long-horizon navigation in unfamiliar environments based on complex linguistic instructions. Its success critically hinges on establishing an efficient ``language-understanding -- visual-perception -- embodied-execution'' closed loop. Existing methods often suffer from perceptual distortion and decision drift in complex, long-distance tasks due to the cognitive overload of a single agent. Inspired by distributed cognition theory, this paper proposes MA-CoNav, a Multi-Agent Collaborative Navigation framework. This framework adopts a ``Master-Slave'' hierarchical agent collaboration architecture, decoupling and distributing the perception, planning, execution, and memory functions required for navigation tasks to specialized agents. Specifically, the Master Agent is responsible for global orchestration, while the Subordinate Agent group collaborates through a clear division of labor: an Observation Agent generates environment descriptions, a Planning Agent performs task decomposition and dynamic verification, an Execution Agent handles simultaneous mapping and action, and a Memory Agent manages structured experiences. Furthermore, the framework introduces a ``Local-Global'' dual-stage reflection mechanism to dynamically optimize the entire navigation pipeline. Empirical experiments were conducted using a real-world indoor dataset collected by a Limo Pro robot, with no scene-specific fine-tuning performed on the models throughout the process. The results demonstrate that MA-CoNav comprehensively outperforms existing mainstream VLN methods across multiple metrics.

</details>


### [197] [Architectural HRI: Towards a Robotic Paradigm Shift in Human-Building Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03052)
*Alex Binh Vinh Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出建筑机器人化将推动人-建筑交互范式转变，使建筑多层物理同步适应居住者需求与可持续目标，同时为HRI知识向非常规机器人形态转移提供理想案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感、通信、界面、控制和机器人技术的进步正在扩展人-建筑交互(HBI)的范围，使其超越自适应建筑服务和立面，向建筑空间的物理驱动发展。同时，机器人化家具、群体机器人和形状可变空间的研究表明，建筑元素现在可以通过机器人增强来实现移动、重新配置和空间适应。这些进展预示着HBI的范式转变，但面临建筑HRI的时间、空间和社会复杂性以及跨学科知识碎片化的挑战。

Method: 论文采用概念分析框架，提出需要跨学科研究来统一建筑形式中机器人驱动的"为什么"、"是什么"和"如何实现"。通过整合HCI、环境心理学、认知科学和建筑学知识，建立建筑机器人化与HRI知识转移的理论基础。

Result: 识别出建筑机器人化将实现建筑多层物理同步适应，更全面地支持居住者需求和可持续目标。同时发现建筑环境为HRI知识向非常规机器人形态（包括将机器人视为多个建筑层甚至整个建筑）转移提供了理想案例。

Conclusion: 呼吁开展跨学科研究，统一建筑形式中机器人驱动的目的、内容和实现方式，以应对建筑HRI的复杂性挑战，推动人-建筑交互向更全面、适应性更强的方向发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in sensing, communication, interfaces, control, and robotics are expanding Human-Building Interaction (HBI) beyond adaptive building services and facades toward the physical actuation of architectural space. In parallel, research in robotic furniture, swarm robotics, and shape-changing spaces shows that architectural elements can now be robotically augmented to move, reconfigure, and adapt space. We propose that these advances promise a paradigm shift in HBI, in which multiple building layers physically adapt in synchrony to support occupant needs and sustainability goals more holistically. Conversely, we argue that this emerging paradigm also provides an ideal case for transferring HRI knowledge to unconventional robotic morphologies, including the interpretation of the robot as multiple architectural layers or even as a building. However, this research agenda remains challenged by the temporal, spatial, and social complexity of architectural HRI, and by fragmented knowledge across HCI, environmental psychology, cognitive science, and architecture. We therefore call for interdisciplinary research that unifies the why, what, and how of robotic actuation in architectural forms.

</details>


### [198] [CMoE: Contrastive Mixture of Experts for Motion Control and Terrain Adaptation of Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03067)
*Shihao Ma,Hongjin Chen,Zijun Xu,Yi Zhao,Ke Wu,Ruichen Yang,Leyao Zou,Zhongxue Gan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CMoE：一种结合对比学习的单阶段强化学习框架，用于提升人形机器人在复杂地形上的自主导航能力，通过优化专家激活分布增强专家专业化，使机器人能够跨越20厘米高台阶和80厘米宽间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在现实环境中需要自主导航各种复杂地形，但传统的混合专家框架在实践中存在专家激活分布均匀的问题，导致专家专业化不足，限制了模型表达能力。

Method: 提出CMoE框架，将对比学习集成到单阶段强化学习中，通过对比约束最大化相同地形内专家激活的一致性，同时最小化不同地形间的相似性，从而鼓励专家专注于特定地形类型。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上的实验表明，CMoE使机器人能够跨越20厘米高连续台阶和80厘米宽间隙，在多样混合地形上实现稳健自然的步态，超越现有方法极限。

Conclusion: CMoE通过对比学习优化专家激活分布，有效提升了混合专家框架在复杂地形导航中的性能，为人形机器人在现实环境中的部署提供了更强大的解决方案，代码已开源以促进社区发展。

Abstract: For effective deployment in real-world environments, humanoid robots must autonomously navigate a diverse range of complex terrains with abrupt transitions. While the Vanilla mixture of experts (MoE) framework is theoretically capable of modeling diverse terrain features, in practice, the gating network exhibits nearly uniform expert activations across different terrains, weakening the expert specialization and limiting the model's expressive power. To address this limitation, we introduce CMoE, a novel single-stage reinforcement learning framework that integrates contrastive learning to refine expert activation distributions. By imposing contrastive constraints, CMoE maximizes the consistency of expert activations within the same terrain while minimizing their similarity across different terrains, thereby encouraging experts to specialize in distinct terrain types. We validated our approach on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot through a series of challenging experiments. Results demonstrate that CMoE enables the robot to traverse continuous steps up to 20 cm high and gaps up to 80 cm wide, while achieving robust and natural gait across diverse mixed terrains, surpassing the limits of existing methods. To support further research and foster community development, we release our code publicly.

</details>


### [199] [Look Forward to Walk Backward: Efficient Terrain Memory for Backward Locomotion with Forward Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03138)
*Shixin Luo,Songbo Li,Yuan Hao,Yaqi Wang,Jun Zheng,Jun Wu,Qiuguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LF2WB框架让腿式机器人利用前向深度相机在前进时构建地形记忆，在后退时检索记忆实现无后视的避障运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人使用前向深度相机时，后退运动缺乏视野预览，纯本体感知控制器在复杂地形上能力受限且会碰撞障碍物

Method: 提出LF2WB地形记忆框架：前进时用前向深度和本体感知写入紧凑关联记忆，后退时检索记忆实现无后视避障；采用delta规则选择性更新，在活动子空间上软移除后写入记忆状态

Result: 模拟和真实场景实验证明该方法有效，在有限感知条件下提升了机器人在复杂地形上的后退敏捷性

Conclusion: LF2WB框架通过地形记忆机制解决了前向相机机器人后退运动的感知限制问题，实现了高效、硬件友好的无后视避障能力

Abstract: Legged robots with egocentric forward-facing depth cameras can couple exteroception and proprioception to achieve robust forward agility on complex terrain. When these robots walk backward, the forward-only field of view provides no preview. Purely proprioceptive controllers can remain stable on moderate ground when moving backward but cannot fully exploit the robot's capabilities on complex terrain and must collide with obstacles. We present Look Forward to Walk Backward (LF2WB), an efficient terrain-memory locomotion framework that uses forward egocentric depth and proprioception to write a compact associative memory during forward motion and to retrieve it for collision-free backward locomotion without rearward vision. The memory backbone employs a delta-rule selective update that softly removes then writes the memory state along the active subspace. Training uses hardware-efficient parallel computation, and deployment runs recurrent, constant-time per-step inference with a constant-size state, making the approach suitable for onboard processors on low-cost robots. Experiments in both simulations and real-world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, improving backward agility across complex terrains under limited sensing.

</details>


### [200] [From Language to Action: Can LLM-Based Agents Be Used for Embodied Robot Cognition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03148)
*Shinas Shaji,Fabian Huppertz,Alex Mitrevski,Sebastian Houben*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM作为认知机器人架构核心组件的潜力，提出一个以LLM为核心、包含工作记忆和情景记忆的认知架构，在模拟家庭环境中评估其规划与执行推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要在日常环境中灵活行动，这需要多种认知能力进行规划和执行恢复。虽然LLM已展现出推理和语言理解等认知能力，但控制具身机器人需要可靠地将高级语言与低级感知控制功能连接起来。本文旨在探索LLM作为认知机器人架构核心组件的潜力。

Method: 提出一个认知架构，其中以LLM作为规划和推理的核心组件，工作记忆和情景记忆组件支持从经验中学习和适应。该架构实例用于控制模拟家庭环境中的移动机械臂，通过一组高级工具（感知、推理、导航、抓取、放置）与环境交互，这些工具都提供给基于LLM的智能体。

Result: 在两项家庭任务（物体放置和物体交换）上评估系统，结果显示LLM驱动的智能体能够完成结构化任务，并展现出适应性涌现和记忆引导规划。但也发现显著局限性：对任务成功的幻觉、拒绝承认和完成顺序任务的指令遵循能力差。

Conclusion: 研究结果突显了将LLM用作自主机器人具身认知控制器的潜力和挑战。LLM能够作为认知架构的核心组件，但在可靠性和指令遵循方面仍需改进。

Abstract: In order to flexibly act in an everyday environment, a robotic agent needs a variety of cognitive capabilities that enable it to reason about plans and perform execution recovery. Large language models (LLMs) have been shown to demonstrate emergent cognitive aspects, such as reasoning and language understanding; however, the ability to control embodied robotic agents requires reliably bridging high-level language to low-level functionalities for perception and control. In this paper, we investigate the extent to which an LLM can serve as a core component for planning and execution reasoning in a cognitive robot architecture. For this purpose, we propose a cognitive architecture in which an agentic LLM serves as the core component for planning and reasoning, while components for working and episodic memories support learning from experience and adaptation. An instance of the architecture is then used to control a mobile manipulator in a simulated household environment, where environment interaction is done through a set of high-level tools for perception, reasoning, navigation, grasping, and placement, all of which are made available to the LLM-based agent. We evaluate our proposed system on two household tasks (object placement and object swapping), which evaluate the agent's reasoning, planning, and memory utilisation. The results demonstrate that the LLM-driven agent can complete structured tasks and exhibits emergent adaptation and memory-guided planning, but also reveal significant limitations, such as hallucinations about the task success and poor instruction following by refusing to acknowledge and complete sequential tasks. These findings highlight both the potential and challenges of employing LLMs as embodied cognitive controllers for autonomous robots.

</details>


### [201] [Robotic Grasping and Placement Controlled by EEG-Based Hybrid Visual and Motor Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03181)
*Yichang Liu,Tianyu Wang,Ziyi Ye,Yawei Li,Yu-Gang Jiang,Shouyan Wang,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个集成EEG视觉与运动想象的机器人控制框架，实现实时意图驱动的抓取和放置任务，通过离线预训练解码器在线零样本应用，建立双通道意图接口


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用BCI驱动机器人技术增强人机交互，将神经信号与物理控制连接，建立直观的意图驱动控制接口

Method: 采用离线预训练解码器在线零样本应用的流式处理管道，建立双通道意图接口：视觉想象识别抓取对象，运动想象确定放置姿态；使用无提示想象协议，仅依赖EEG信号

Result: 在线解码准确率：视觉想象40.23%，运动想象62.59%；端到端任务成功率20.88%；在遮挡目标、不同参与者姿势等多样化场景中验证系统有效性

Conclusion: 证明了高级视觉认知可以实时解码并转换为可执行机器人指令，弥合了神经信号与物理交互之间的差距，验证了纯基于想象的BCI范式在实际人机协作中的灵活性

Abstract: We present a framework that integrates EEG-based visual and motor imagery (VI/MI) with robotic control to enable real-time, intention-driven grasping and placement. Motivated by the promise of BCI-driven robotics to enhance human-robot interaction, this system bridges neural signals with physical control by deploying offline-pretrained decoders in a zero-shot manner within an online streaming pipeline. This establishes a dual-channel intent interface that translates visual intent into robotic actions, with VI identifying objects for grasping and MI determining placement poses, enabling intuitive control over both what to grasp and where to place. The system operates solely on EEG via a cue-free imagery protocol, achieving integration and online validation. Implemented on a Base robotic platform and evaluated across diverse scenarios, including occluded targets or varying participant postures, the system achieves online decoding accuracies of 40.23% (VI) and 62.59% (MI), with an end-to-end task success rate of 20.88%. These results demonstrate that high-level visual cognition can be decoded in real time and translated into executable robot commands, bridging the gap between neural signals and physical interaction, and validating the flexibility of a purely imagery-based BCI paradigm for practical human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [202] [ACE-Brain-0: Spatial Intelligence as a Shared Scaffold for Universal Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03198)
*Ziyang Gong,Zehang Luo,Anke Tang,Zhe Liu,Shi Fu,Zhi Hou,Ganlin Yang,Weiyun Wang,Xiaofeng Wang,Jianbo Liu,Gen Luo,Haolan Kang,Shuang Luo,Yue Zhou,Yong Luo,Li Shen,Xiaosong Jia,Yao Mu,Xue Yang,Chunxiao Liu,Junchi Yan,Hengshuang Zhao,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACE-Brain-0是一个通用基础大脑，通过空间智能作为跨异构体化（自动驾驶、机器人、无人机）的统一支架，采用SSR范式（支架-专业化-协调）和GRPO优化，在24个基准测试中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体化智能模型在训练统一模型时面临长尾数据、梯度干扰和灾难性遗忘等问题，难以平衡通用泛化与领域专精。需要一种能够统一空间推理、自动驾驶和体化操作的方法。

Method: 提出SSR范式：1）建立共享空间基础（支架）；2）培养领域专业化专家；3）通过无数据模型融合协调专家。采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)增强模型综合能力。基于空间智能作为跨异构体化的通用支架。

Result: 在24个空间和体化相关基准测试中，ACE-Brain-0取得了竞争性甚至最先进的性能表现，证明了其跨异构体化的有效泛化能力。

Conclusion: 空间智能可以作为跨异构体化的通用支架，SSR范式能够有效解决统一模型训练中的挑战，ACE-Brain-0展示了在多样化体化任务上的强大泛化能力。

Abstract: Universal embodied intelligence demands robust generalization across heterogeneous embodiments, such as autonomous driving, robotics, and unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). However, existing embodied brain in training a unified model over diverse embodiments frequently triggers long-tail data, gradient interference, and catastrophic forgetting, making it notoriously difficult to balance universal generalization with domain-specific proficiency. In this report, we introduce ACE-Brain-0, a generalist foundation brain that unifies spatial reasoning, autonomous driving, and embodied manipulation within a single multimodal large language model~(MLLM). Our key insight is that spatial intelligence serves as a universal scaffold across diverse physical embodiments: although vehicles, robots, and UAVs differ drastically in morphology, they share a common need for modeling 3D mental space, making spatial cognition a natural, domain-agnostic foundation for cross-embodiment transfer. Building on this insight, we propose the Scaffold-Specialize-Reconcile~(SSR) paradigm, which first establishes a shared spatial foundation, then cultivates domain-specialized experts, and finally harmonizes them through data-free model merging. Furthermore, we adopt Group Relative Policy Optimization~(GRPO) to strengthen the model's comprehensive capability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ACE-Brain-0 achieves competitive and even state-of-the-art performance across 24 spatial and embodiment-related benchmarks.

</details>


### [203] [HoMMI: Learning Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03243)
*Xiaomeng Xu,Jisang Park,Han Zhang,Eric Cousineau,Aditya Bhat,Jose Barreiros,Dian Wang,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HoMMI是一个从无机器人人类演示中学习全身移动操作的数据收集与策略学习框架，通过增强UMI接口的自我中心感知来捕获移动操作所需的全局上下文，并设计跨具身手眼策略来弥合人机具身差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作需要全局上下文感知，但现有UMI接口缺乏自我中心感知能力；同时，将自我中心感知引入后扩大了人机具身差距，使得策略迁移变得困难。

Method: 1) 在UMI接口中增强自我中心感知以捕获全局上下文；2) 设计跨具身手眼策略，包括具身无关的视觉表示、松弛的头部动作表示，以及考虑机器人物理约束的全身控制器来实现手眼轨迹协调。

Result: 框架能够实现需要双手协调、全身协调、导航和主动感知的长时程移动操作任务，实现了便携、无机器人、可扩展的数据收集。

Conclusion: HoMMI通过增强自我中心感知和跨具身策略设计，成功解决了移动操作中的全局上下文捕获和人机具身差距问题，为从人类演示中学习复杂移动操作任务提供了有效框架。

Abstract: We present Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation Interface (HoMMI), a data collection and policy learning framework that learns whole-body mobile manipulation directly from robot-free human demonstrations. We augment UMI interfaces with egocentric sensing to capture the global context required for mobile manipulation, enabling portable, robot-free, and scalable data collection. However, naively incorporating egocentric sensing introduces a larger human-to-robot embodiment gap in both observation and action spaces, making policy transfer difficult. We explicitly bridge this gap with a cross-embodiment hand-eye policy design, including an embodiment agnostic visual representation; a relaxed head action representation; and a whole-body controller that realizes hand-eye trajectories through coordinated whole-body motion under robot-specific physical constraints. Together, these enable long-horizon mobile manipulation tasks requiring bimanual and whole-body coordination, navigation, and active perception. Results are best viewed on: https://hommi-robot.github.io

</details>


### [204] [Tether: Autonomous Functional Play with Correspondence-Driven Trajectory Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03278)
*William Liang,Sam Wang,Hung-Ju Wang,Osbert Bastani,Yecheng Jason Ma,Dinesh Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Tether方法通过语义关键点对应实现数据高效的动作变换，结合视觉语言模型指导的自主功能游戏循环，从少量演示生成多样化高质量数据集，提升闭环模仿策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人通过交互和经验学习的问题，替代劳动密集型人工演示，需要(1)对多样化、可能分布外环境状态具有鲁棒性的策略，(2)持续产生有用机器人经验的过程。

Method: 设计基于语义关键点对应的开环策略，将少量源演示动作变换到目标场景；通过任务选择、执行、评估和改进的自主功能游戏循环，利用视觉语言模型指导生成多样化高质量数据集。

Result: 在家庭式多物体设置中首次实现从仅少量演示开始的长时间自主多任务游戏，生成超过1000条专家级轨迹，训练出的策略与人类收集演示学习的策略具有竞争力。

Conclusion: Tether方法实现了数据高效的自主功能游戏，通过结构化任务导向交互产生持续改进的机器人经验流，显著提升闭环模仿策略性能，为机器人学习提供了可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: The ability to conduct and learn from interaction and experience is a central challenge in robotics, offering a scalable alternative to labor-intensive human demonstrations. However, realizing such "play" requires (1) a policy robust to diverse, potentially out-of-distribution environment states, and (2) a procedure that continuously produces useful robot experience. To address these challenges, we introduce Tether, a method for autonomous functional play involving structured, task-directed interactions. First, we design a novel open-loop policy that warps actions from a small set of source demonstrations (<=10) by anchoring them to semantic keypoint correspondences in the target scene. We show that this design is extremely data-efficient and robust even under significant spatial and semantic variations. Second, we deploy this policy for autonomous functional play in the real world via a continuous cycle of task selection, execution, evaluation, and improvement, guided by the visual understanding capabilities of vision-language models. This procedure generates diverse, high-quality datasets with minimal human intervention. In a household-like multi-object setup, our method is the first to perform many hours of autonomous multi-task play in the real world starting from only a handful of demonstrations. This produces a stream of data that consistently improves the performance of closed-loop imitation policies over time, ultimately yielding over 1000 expert-level trajectories and training policies competitive with those learned from human-collected demonstrations.

</details>


### [205] [ULTRA: Unified Multimodal Control for Autonomous Humanoid Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03279)
*Xialin He,Sirui Xu,Xinyao Li,Runpei Dong,Liuyu Bian,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ULTRA：一个统一的框架，通过物理驱动的神经重定向和统一多模态控制器，实现从稀疏任务规范到自主全身运动操作的生成，无需测试时参考运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在三个主要限制：重定向数据稀缺或质量低；难以扩展到大规模技能库；依赖跟踪预定义运动参考而非从感知和高级任务规范生成行为。这些限制阻碍了人形机器人实现自主、多功能的全身运动操作。

Method: 提出ULTRA框架，包含两个关键组件：1）物理驱动的神经重定向算法，将大规模运动捕捉数据转换为物理合理的人形机器人运动，特别关注接触丰富的交互；2）统一多模态控制器，支持密集参考和稀疏任务规范，适应从精确运动捕捉状态到噪声自我中心视觉输入的多种感知模式。通过将通用跟踪策略蒸馏到控制器中，将运动技能压缩到紧凑的潜在空间，并应用强化学习微调以扩展覆盖范围并提高分布外场景的鲁棒性。

Result: 在仿真和真实Unitree G1人形机器人上评估ULTRA。结果显示，ULTRA能够从自我中心感知实现自主、目标条件的全身运动操作，在有限技能情况下始终优于仅跟踪的基线方法。

Conclusion: ULTRA框架通过结合物理驱动的重定向和统一多模态控制，实现了从稀疏意图到协调全身行为的生成，无需测试时参考运动，为人形机器人的实际应用提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Achieving autonomous and versatile whole-body loco-manipulation remains a central barrier to making humanoids practically useful. Yet existing approaches are fundamentally constrained: retargeted data are often scarce or low-quality; methods struggle to scale to large skill repertoires; and, most importantly, they rely on tracking predefined motion references rather than generating behavior from perception and high-level task specifications. To address these limitations, we propose ULTRA, a unified framework with two key components. First, we introduce a physics-driven neural retargeting algorithm that translates large-scale motion capture to humanoid embodiments while preserving physical plausibility for contact-rich interactions. Second, we learn a unified multimodal controller that supports both dense references and sparse task specifications, under sensing ranging from accurate motion-capture state to noisy egocentric visual inputs. We distill a universal tracking policy into this controller, compress motor skills into a compact latent space, and apply reinforcement learning finetuning to expand coverage and improve robustness under out-of-distribution scenarios. This enables coordinated whole-body behavior from sparse intent without test-time reference motions. We evaluate ULTRA in simulation and on a real Unitree G1 humanoid. Results show that ULTRA generalizes to autonomous, goal-conditioned whole-body loco-manipulation from egocentric perception, consistently outperforming tracking-only baselines with limited skills.

</details>


### [206] [How to Peel with a Knife: Aligning Fine-Grained Manipulation with Human Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03280)
*Toru Lin,Shuying Deng,Zhao-Heng Yin,Pieter Abbeel,Jitendra Malik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段学习框架，通过力感知数据收集和模仿学习获得初始策略，再通过偏好微调结合定量任务指标和人类反馈，解决接触丰富、力敏感且具有隐式成功标准的复杂操作任务（以削皮为例）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多基本操作任务（如食物准备、手术、手工艺）对自主机器人仍然难以实现。这些任务不仅具有接触丰富、力敏感的动力学特性，更重要的是具有"隐式"成功标准：任务质量是连续且主观的（如土豆削皮的好坏），难以进行定量评估和奖励设计。

Method: 采用两阶段学习框架：1）通过力感知数据收集和模仿学习学习鲁棒的初始策略，实现跨物体变化的泛化；2）通过基于偏好的微调，使用结合定量任务指标和定性人类反馈的学习奖励模型，使策略行为与人类对任务质量的概念对齐。

Result: 仅使用50-200个削皮轨迹，系统在黄瓜、苹果、土豆等挑战性农产品上实现超过90%的平均成功率，通过偏好微调性能提升高达40%。在单一农产品类别上训练的策略对未见类别内实例和分布外不同类别农产品表现出强大的零样本泛化能力，同时保持超过90%的成功率。

Conclusion: 该学习框架成功解决了具有隐式成功标准的接触丰富操作任务，通过结合模仿学习和偏好微调，实现了对复杂操作任务的有效学习，并展示了强大的泛化能力，为自主机器人执行类似任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Many essential manipulation tasks - such as food preparation, surgery, and craftsmanship - remain intractable for autonomous robots. These tasks are characterized not only by contact-rich, force-sensitive dynamics, but also by their "implicit" success criteria: unlike pick-and-place, task quality in these domains is continuous and subjective (e.g. how well a potato is peeled), making quantitative evaluation and reward engineering difficult. We present a learning framework for such tasks, using peeling with a knife as a representative example. Our approach follows a two-stage pipeline: first, we learn a robust initial policy via force-aware data collection and imitation learning, enabling generalization across object variations; second, we refine the policy through preference-based finetuning using a learned reward model that combines quantitative task metrics with qualitative human feedback, aligning policy behavior with human notions of task quality. Using only 50-200 peeling trajectories, our system achieves over 90% average success rates on challenging produce including cucumbers, apples, and potatoes, with performance improving by up to 40% through preference-based finetuning. Remarkably, policies trained on a single produce category exhibit strong zero-shot generalization to unseen in-category instances and to out-of-distribution produce from different categories while maintaining over 90% success rates.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [Federated Inference: Toward Privacy-Preserving Collaborative and Incentivized Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02214)
*Jungwon Seo,Ferhat Ozgur Catak,Chunming Rong,Jaeyeon Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 联邦推理(FI)作为一种独立的协作范式，研究独立训练、私有拥有的模型如何在推理时不共享数据或参数进行协作，强调推理时隐私保护与协作性能增益两个基本要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究从不同角度探索了安全分布式推理，但缺乏对联邦推理的统一抽象和系统级理解。需要建立FI作为与联邦学习互补的协作范式，理解其核心设计维度和系统特性。

Method: 将FI形式化为受保护的协作计算，分析其核心设计维度，研究隐私约束、非独立同分布数据和有限可观测性在推理时共同作用下的结构权衡。通过具体实例化和实证分析，考察隐私保护推理、基于集成的协作和激励机制对齐中的摩擦点。

Result: 研究发现FI展现出无法直接从训练时联邦或经典集成方法继承的系统级行为。识别了隐私保护推理、集成协作和激励机制对齐中的重复摩擦点，表明FI具有独特的系统特性。

Conclusion: FI是与联邦学习互补的独立协作范式，需要解决推理时隐私保护、协作性能增益等基本要求。本文提供了FI的统一视角，并指出了实现实用、可扩展、隐私保护的协作推理系统必须解决的开放挑战。

Abstract: Federated Inference (FI) studies how independently trained and privately owned models can collaborate at inference time without sharing data or model parameters. While recent work has explored secure and distributed inference from disparate perspectives, a unified abstraction and system-level understanding of FI remain lacking. This paper positions FI as a distinct collaborative paradigm, complementary to federated learning, and identifies two fundamental requirements that govern its feasibility: inference-time privacy preservation and meaningful performance gains through collaboration. We formalize FI as a protected collaborative computation, analyze its core design dimensions, and examine the structural trade-offs that arise when privacy constraints, non-IID data, and limited observability are jointly imposed at inference time. Through a concrete instantiation and empirical analysis, we highlight recurring friction points in privacy-preserving inference, ensemble-based collaboration, and incentive alignment. Our findings suggest that FI exhibits system-level behaviors that cannot be directly inherited from training-time federation or classical ensemble methods. Overall, this work provides a unifying perspective on FI and outlines open challenges that must be addressed to enable practical, scalable, and privacy-preserving collaborative inference systems.

</details>


### [208] [Estimating Visual Attribute Effects in Advertising from Observational Data: A Deepfake-Informed Double Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02359)
*Yizhi Liu,Balaji Padmanabhan,Siva Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DICE-DML框架，利用生成式AI解决图像中视觉属性因果效应估计的混杂偏倚问题，相比标准DML方法显著提升估计准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字广告依赖视觉内容，但缺乏严谨方法估计图像中特定视觉属性（如肤色）对消费者参与度的因果效应。标准DML方法因视觉编码器将处理信息与混杂变量纠缠而产生严重偏倚估计。

Method: 开发DICE-DML框架，结合三种机制：1) 使用深度伪造生成的图像对隔离处理变异；2) 在配对差异向量上进行DICE-Diff对抗学习，背景信号抵消以揭示纯处理指纹；3) 正交投影几何移除处理轴分量。

Result: 在已知真实值的模拟中，DICE-DML相比标准DML将均方根误差降低73-97%，在零效应点改进最强（97.5%）。应用于232,089个Instagram影响者帖子，标准DML产生诊断无效结果（负结果R^2），而DICE-DML实现有效混杂控制（R^2=0.63），估计肤色较深有边际显著负面效应（-522个赞；p=0.062）。

Conclusion: DICE-DML为处理与混杂变量共存于图像中的视觉数据因果推断提供了原则性方法，显著改善估计准确性并控制I类错误。

Abstract: Digital advertising increasingly relies on visual content, yet marketers lack rigorous methods for understanding how specific visual attributes causally affect consumer engagement. This paper addresses a fundamental methodological challenge: estimating causal effects when the treatment, such as a model's skin tone, is an attribute embedded within the image itself. Standard approaches like Double Machine Learning (DML) fail in this setting because vision encoders entangle treatment information with confounding variables, producing severely biased estimates. We develop DICE-DML (Deepfake-Informed Control Encoder for Double Machine Learning), a framework that leverages generative AI to disentangle treatment from confounders. The approach combines three mechanisms: (1) deepfake-generated image pairs that isolate treatment variation; (2) DICE-Diff adversarial learning on paired difference vectors, where background signals cancel to reveal pure treatment fingerprints; and (3) orthogonal projection that geometrically removes treatment-axis components. In simulations with known ground truth, DICE-DML reduces root mean squared error by 73-97% compared to standard DML, with the strongest improvement (97.5%) at the null effect point, demonstrating robust Type I error control. Applying DICE-DML to 232,089 Instagram influencer posts, we estimate the causal effect of skin tone on engagement. Standard DML produces diagnostically invalid results (negative outcome R^2), while DICE-DML achieves valid confounding control (R^2 = 0.63) and estimates a marginally significant negative effect of darker skin tone (-522 likes; p = 0.062), substantially smaller than the biased standard estimate. Our framework provides a principled approach for causal inference with visual data when treatments and confounders coexist within images.

</details>


### [209] [Can machines be uncertain?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02365)
*Luis Rosa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨AI系统如何实现不确定性状态，区分了数据固有的认知不确定性和系统主观的不确定性态度，并提出了不确定性可作为疑问态度的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI系统是否以及如何能够实现不确定性状态，从功能主义和行为主义视角分析不同AI架构（符号、连接、混合）如何容纳不确定性。

Method: 采用功能主义和行为主义视角，分析符号、连接和混合架构；区分认知不确定性和主观不确定性；区分分布式和离散实现；提出不确定性作为疑问态度的概念框架。

Result: 建立了AI系统中不确定性的分类框架：认知不确定性（数据固有）vs主观不确定性（系统态度）；分布式vs离散实现；关键贡献是提出某些不确定性状态是疑问态度，其内容是问题而非命题。

Conclusion: AI系统可以实现不确定性状态，不同架构以不同方式容纳不确定性；不确定性可作为疑问态度，这对AI系统的认知建模和决策过程有重要理论意义。

Abstract: The paper investigates whether and how AI systems can realize states of uncertainty. By adopting a functionalist and behavioral perspective, it examines how symbolic, connectionist and hybrid architectures make room for uncertainty. The paper distinguishes between epistemic uncertainty, or uncertainty inherent in the data or information, and subjective uncertainty, or the system's own attitude of being uncertainty. It further distinguishes between distributed and discrete realizations of subjective uncertainty. A key contribution is the idea that some states of uncertainty are interrogative attitudes whose content is a question rather than a proposition.

</details>


### [210] [VL-KGE: Vision-Language Models Meet Knowledge Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02435)
*Athanasios Efthymiou,Stevan Rudinac,Monika Kackovic,Nachoem Wijnberg,Marcel Worring*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VL-KGE框架将视觉语言模型的跨模态对齐能力与知识图谱嵌入的结构化关系建模相结合，学习统一的多模态知识图谱表示，在链接预测任务上优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的多模态知识图谱本质上是异构的，包含多种模态的实体关联。传统知识图谱嵌入方法主要针对单模态设计，现有多模态方法存在模态孤立处理、跨模态对齐弱、假设模态可用性均匀等局限。视觉语言模型提供了在共享嵌入空间中对齐不同模态的强大能力。

Method: 提出Vision-Language Knowledge Graph Embeddings (VL-KGE)框架，整合视觉语言模型的跨模态对齐能力和结构化关系建模，学习统一的多模态知识图谱表示。该方法利用VLMs将不同模态对齐到共享嵌入空间，同时保持知识图谱的结构化关系信息。

Result: 在WN9-IMG和两个新的美术多模态知识图谱WikiArt-MKG-v1和WikiArt-MKG-v2上的实验表明，VL-KGE在链接预测任务上持续优于传统的单模态和多模态KGE方法。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型对多模态知识图谱嵌入具有重要价值，能够在大规模异构知识图谱上实现更鲁棒和结构化的推理。VL-KGE框架成功整合了跨模态对齐和结构化关系建模的优势。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal knowledge graphs (MKGs) are inherently heterogeneous, modeling entities that are associated with diverse modalities. Traditional knowledge graph embedding (KGE) methods excel at learning continuous representations of entities and relations, yet they are typically designed for unimodal settings. Recent approaches extend KGE to multimodal settings but remain constrained, often processing modalities in isolation, resulting in weak cross-modal alignment, and relying on simplistic assumptions such as uniform modality availability across entities. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer a powerful way to align diverse modalities within a shared embedding space. We propose Vision-Language Knowledge Graph Embeddings (VL-KGE), a framework that integrates cross-modal alignment from VLMs with structured relational modeling to learn unified multimodal representations of knowledge graphs. Experiments on WN9-IMG and two novel fine art MKGs, WikiArt-MKG-v1 and WikiArt-MKG-v2, demonstrate that VL-KGE consistently improves over traditional unimodal and multimodal KGE methods in link prediction tasks. Our results highlight the value of VLMs for multimodal KGE, enabling more robust and structured reasoning over large-scale heterogeneous knowledge graphs.

</details>


### [211] [A Neuropsychologically Grounded Evaluation of LLM Cognitive Abilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02540)
*Faiz Ghifari Haznitrama,Faeyza Rishad Ardi,Alice Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过引入基于神经心理学测试的NeuroCognition基准，揭示LLMs在抽象推理、工作记忆和认知灵活性等基础认知能力上的不足，尽管它们在传统文本任务上表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型在统一能力因子方面表现出色，但在人类看来简单的任务上却表现不佳。现有基准主要关注任务完成度，未能探测揭示这些行为的基础认知能力，因此需要开发新的评估框架。

Method: 引入NeuroCognition基准，基于三个改编的神经心理学测试：Raven渐进矩阵（抽象关系推理）、空间工作记忆（维持和系统搜索）和威斯康星卡片分类测试（认知灵活性）。对156个模型进行因子分析，评估模型在不同模态（文本、图像）和复杂度下的表现。

Result: 模型在文本任务上表现良好，但在图像任务和复杂度增加时性能下降。复杂推理并非普遍有益，而简单的人类策略能带来部分提升。NeuroCognition与标准能力基准正相关，但测量了超越它们的独特认知能力。

Conclusion: NeuroCognition揭示了当前LLMs在哪些方面与人类智能一致，在哪些方面缺乏核心适应性认知，有潜力作为改进LLMs的可验证、可扩展的评估来源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit a unified "general factor" of capability across 10 benchmarks, a finding confirmed by our factor analysis of 156 models, yet they still struggle with simple, trivial tasks for humans. This is because current benchmarks focus on task completion, failing to probe the foundational cognitive abilities that highlight these behaviors. We address this by introducing the NeuroCognition benchmark, grounded in three adapted neuropsychological tests: Raven's Progressive Matrices (abstract relational reasoning), Spatial Working Memory (maintenance and systematic search), and the Wisconsin Card Sorting Test (cognitive flexibility). Our evaluation reveals that while models perform strongly on text, their performance degrades for images and with increased complexity. Furthermore, we observe that complex reasoning is not universally beneficial, whereas simple, human-like strategies yield partial gains. We also find that NeuroCognition correlates positively with standard general-capability benchmarks, while still measuring distinct cognitive abilities beyond them. Overall, NeuroCognition emphasizes where current LLMs align with human-like intelligence and where they lack core adaptive cognition, showing the potential to serve as a verifiable, scalable source for improving LLMs.

</details>


### [212] [SUN: Shared Use of Next-token Prediction for Efficient Multi-LLM Disaggregated Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02599)
*Sunghyeon Woo,Ahreum Seo,Jaegwang Lee,Jaeeun Kil,Hanbae Seo,Joonghoon Kim,Baeseong Park,Se Jung Kwon,Dongsoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SUN通过解耦预填充和解码模块，实现多LLM服务中跨模型共享解码执行，显著提升GPU利用率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模型LLM服务中，由于模型特定资源分区导致解码执行效率低下，跨模型批处理不可行，内存受限的解码在倾斜工作负载下GPU利用率严重不足

Method: 将仅解码器Transformer分解为预填充模块和解码模块，仅微调任务特定的预填充模块，使冻结的解码模块可在不同模型间共享，实现模型无关的解码路由策略

Result: SUN在保持与全微调相当精度的同时，用更少解码工作器维持系统吞吐量，GPU吞吐量提升达2.0倍，每输出token时间(TPOT)保持在5%以内；量化版QSUN实现45%加速

Conclusion: SUN是首个在解耦多LLM服务中实现跨模型解码执行共享的方法，显著提升GPU利用率，为低比特解码提供支持，平衡了精度与效率

Abstract: In multi-model LLM serving, decode execution remains inefficient due to model-specific resource partitioning: since cross-model batching is not possible, memory-bound decoding often suffers from severe GPU underutilization, especially under skewed workloads. We propose Shared Use of Next-token Prediction (SUN), the first approach that enables cross-model sharing of decode execution in disaggregated multi-LLM serving. SUN decomposes a decoder-only Transformer into a prefill module and a decode module, and fine-tunes only the task-specific prefill module, enabling a frozen decode module to be shared across models. This design enables a model-agnostic decode routing policy that balances decode requests across shared workers to maximize utilization. Across diverse tasks and model families, SUN achieves accuracy comparable to full fine-tuning while maintaining system throughput with fewer decode workers. In particular, SUN improves throughput per GPU by up to 2.0x over conventional disaggregation while keeping time-per-output-token (TPOT) within 5%. SUN inherently enables and facilitates low-bit decoding; with Quantized SUN (QSUN), it achieves a 45% speedup with comparable accuracy to SUN while preserving the benefits of shared decoding.

</details>


### [213] [AgentAssay: Token-Efficient Regression Testing for Non-Deterministic AI Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02601)
*Varun Pratap Bhardwaj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentAssay：首个用于AI智能体回归测试的token高效框架，通过行为指纹、统计假设检验和离线分析等技术，实现78-100%的成本降低，同时保持严格的统计保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体大规模部署，但缺乏系统化的方法来验证智能体在提示、工具、模型或编排逻辑变更后是否发生回归。现有方法成本高且难以处理非确定性智能体工作流。

Method: 提出AgentAssay框架，包含8个核心技术：1) 基于假设检验的随机三值判定；2) 五维智能体覆盖度量；3) 智能体特定变异测试算子；4) 智能体工作流的蜕变关系；5) CI/CD部署门控作为统计决策过程；6) 行为指纹技术将执行轨迹映射到紧凑向量；7) 自适应预算优化根据行为方差校准试验次数；8) 基于轨迹的离线分析实现零成本测试。

Result: 在5个模型(GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Mistral-Large-3、Llama-4-Maverick、Phi-4)、3个场景、7,605次试验中，行为指纹检测能力达86%(二进制测试为0%)，SPRT减少78%试验次数，完整流水线通过轨迹优先分析实现100%成本节省。

Conclusion: AgentAssay为AI智能体回归测试提供了首个系统化、token高效的解决方案，通过统计保证、行为分析和成本优化，解决了智能体部署中的回归验证难题，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents are deployed at unprecedented scale, yet no principled methodology exists for
  verifying that an agent has not regressed after changes to its prompts, tools, models, or
  orchestration logic. We present AgentAssay, the first token-efficient framework for regression
  testing non-deterministic AI agent workflows, achieving 78-100% cost reduction while maintaining
  rigorous statistical guarantees. Our contributions include: (1) stochastic three-valued verdicts
  (PASS/FAIL/INCONCLUSIVE) grounded in hypothesis testing; (2) five-dimensional agent coverage metrics;
  (3) agent-specific mutation testing operators; (4) metamorphic relations for agent workflows; (5)
  CI/CD deployment gates as statistical decision procedures; (6) behavioral fingerprinting that maps
  execution traces to compact vectors, enabling multivariate regression detection; (7) adaptive budget
  optimization calibrating trial counts to behavioral variance; and (8) trace-first offline analysis
  enabling zero-cost testing on production traces. Experiments across 5 models (GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet
  4.6, Mistral-Large-3, Llama-4-Maverick, Phi-4), 3 scenarios, and 7,605 trials demonstrate that
  behavioral fingerprinting achieves 86% detection power where binary testing has 0%, SPRT reduces
  trials by 78%, and the full pipeline achieves 100% cost savings through trace-first analysis.
  Implementation: 20,000+ lines of Python, 751 tests, 10 framework adapters.

</details>


### [214] [See and Remember: A Multimodal Agent for Web Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02626)
*Xinjun Wang,Shengyao Wang,Aimin Zhou,Hao Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: V-GEMS是一种用于自主网页导航的多模态智能体架构，通过视觉定位和显式记忆系统解决空间迷失和导航循环问题，显著提升网页遍历性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型的智能体在自主网页导航中面临空间迷失和导航循环的挑战，需要更精确和鲁棒的架构来处理复杂的视觉环境和长期上下文。

Method: 提出V-GEMS架构，集成视觉定位来解决模糊交互元素，引入带有状态跟踪的显式记忆栈，维护结构化遍历路径地图，支持有效回溯并防止深度导航任务中的循环失败。

Result: 实验显示V-GEMS显著优于WebWalker基线，实现了28.7%的性能提升，并引入了可更新的动态基准来严格评估适应性。

Conclusion: V-GEMS通过视觉定位和显式记忆系统的双重机制，为自主网页导航提供了精确且鲁棒的解决方案，有效解决了空间迷失和导航循环问题。

Abstract: Autonomous web navigation requires agents to perceive complex visual environments and maintain long-term context, yet current Large Language Model (LLM) based agents often struggle with spatial disorientation and navigation loops. In this paper, we propose generally applicable V-GEMS(Visual Grounding and Explicit Memory System), a robust multimodal agent architecture designed for precise and resilient web traversal. Our agent integrates visual grounding to resolve ambiguous interactive elements and introduces an explicit memory stack with state tracking. This dual mechanism allows the agent to maintain a structured map of its traversal path, enabling valid backtracking and preventing cyclical failures in deep navigation tasks. We also introduce an updatable dynamic benchmark to rigorously evaluate adaptability. Experiments show V-GEMS significantly dominates the WebWalker baseline, achieving a substantial 28.7% performance gain. Code is available at https://github.com/Vaultttttttttttt/V-GEMS.

</details>


### [215] [LLMs for High-Frequency Decision-Making: Normalized Action Reward-Guided Consistency Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02680)
*Yang Zhao,Zihao Li,Zhiyu Jiang,Dandan Ma,Ganchao Liu,Wenzhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NAR-CP方法解决LLM在高频决策任务中的局限性，通过归一化动作奖励和一致性策略优化提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的决策方法主要针对低频、状态空间语义差异大的离散任务（如家庭规划），在高频决策任务中表现有限。高频任务中高精度数值状态信息频繁更新且波动小，且存在子任务与复合任务间的策略不对齐问题。

Method: 提出归一化动作奖励引导的一致性策略优化（NAR-CP）：1）从候选动作的环境反馈中获取预定义密集奖励，通过归一化完成奖励塑形，理论上验证动作奖励归一化不损害最优策略；2）使用LLM推断子观测候选动作并生成联合策略，通过一致性损失确保全局语义策略与子语义策略的精确对齐。

Result: 在典型高频任务UAV追捕上的实验表明，该方法在独立和复合任务上均表现出优越性能，并对未见任务具有优秀的泛化能力。

Conclusion: NAR-CP方法有效解决了LLM在高频决策任务中的局限性，通过归一化奖励机制和一致性策略优化，提升了在高频、数值状态频繁更新场景下的决策性能，并具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) form the cornerstone of sequential decision-making agent development, they have inherent limitations in high-frequency decision tasks. Existing research mainly focuses on discrete embodied decision scenarios with low-frequency and significant semantic differences in state space (e.g., household planning). These methods suffer from limited performance in high-frequency decision-making tasks, since high-precision numerical state information in such tasks undergoes frequent updates with minimal fluctuations, and exhibiting policy misalignment between the learned sub-tasks and composite tasks. To address these issues, this paper proposes Normalized Action Reward guided Consistency Policy Optimization (NAR-CP). 1) Our method first acquires predefined dense rewards from environmental feedback of candidate actions via reward functions, then completes reward shaping through normalization, and theoretically verifies action reward normalization does not impair optimal policy. 2) To reduce policy misalignment in composite tasks, we use LLMs to infer sub-observation candidate actions and generate joint policies, with consistency loss ensuring precise alignment between global semantic policies and sub-semantic policies. Experiments on UAV pursuit, a typical high-frequency task, show our method delivers superior performance on independent and composite tasks with excellent generalization to unseen tasks.

</details>


### [216] [Retrieval-Augmented Robots via Retrieve-Reason-Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02688)
*Izat Temiraliev,Diji Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出检索增强机器人学(RAR)范式，使机器人能够主动检索外部视觉程序文档来填补零样本任务中的信息缺口，通过检索-推理-执行的循环实现复杂装配任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在零样本设置下，机器人面临关键信息缺口（如复杂家具装配的确切步骤），这些信息无法通过内部参数知识或记忆获得。现有方法主要检索过去运动轨迹或文本安全规则，无法满足主动任务构建的核心需求——从未见的外部非结构化文档中获取程序知识。

Method: 提出检索增强机器人学(RAR)范式，将任务执行建模为迭代的检索-推理-执行循环：机器人主动从非结构化语料库检索相关视觉程序手册，通过跨模态对齐将抽象2D图与3D物理部件关联，并合成可执行计划。

Result: 在具有挑战性的长视野装配基准测试中，基于检索视觉文档的机器人规划显著优于依赖零样本推理或少量示例检索的基线方法。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了RAR的基础，将信息检索的范围从回答用户查询扩展到驱动具身物理行动，使机器人从被动执行者演变为主动信息检索用户。

Abstract: To achieve general-purpose utility, we argue that robots must evolve from passive executors into active Information Retrieval users. In strictly zero-shot settings where no prior demonstrations exist, robots face a critical information gap, such as the exact sequence required to assemble a complex furniture kit, that cannot be satisfied by internal parametric knowledge (common sense) or past internal memory. While recent robotic works attempt to use search before action, they primarily focus on retrieving past kinematic trajectories (analogous to searching internal memory) or text-based safety rules (searching for constraints). These approaches fail to address the core information need of active task construction: acquiring unseen procedural knowledge from external, unstructured documentation. In this paper, we define the paradigm as Retrieval-Augmented Robotics (RAR), empowering the robot with the information-seeking capability that bridges the gap between visual documentation and physical actuation. We formulate the task execution as an iterative Retrieve-Reason-Act loop: the robot or embodied agent actively retrieves relevant visual procedural manuals from an unstructured corpus, grounds the abstract 2D diagrams to 3D physical parts via cross-modal alignment, and synthesizes executable plans. We validate this paradigm on a challenging long-horizon assembly benchmark. Our experiments demonstrate that grounding robotic planning in retrieved visual documents significantly outperforms baselines relying on zero-shot reasoning or few-shot example retrieval. This work establishes the basis of RAR, extending the scope of Information Retrieval from answering user queries to driving embodied physical actions.

</details>


### [217] [FinTexTS: Financial Text-Paired Time-Series Dataset via Semantic-Based and Multi-Level Pairing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02702)
*Jaehoon Lee,Suhwan Park,Tae Yoon Lim,Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,SoonYoung Lee,Yongjae Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出语义和多层次配对框架FinTexTS，通过SEC文件提取公司上下文，使用嵌入匹配检索相关新闻，并用LLM将新闻分为四个层次，构建大规模文本配对股票价格数据集，提升股价预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融领域存在复杂的相互依赖关系，公司股价不仅受自身事件影响，还受其他公司和宏观经济因素影响。现有基于简单关键词匹配的文本-时间序列配对方法无法捕捉这种复杂关系，需要更精细的配对框架。

Method: 提出语义和多层次配对框架：1) 从SEC文件中提取目标公司的特定上下文；2) 使用基于嵌入的匹配机制检索语义相关的新闻文章；3) 利用大语言模型(LLMs)将新闻文章分为四个层次：宏观层面、行业层面、相关公司层面和目标公司层面；4) 应用该框架构建FinTexTS数据集。

Result: 构建了FinTexTS大规模文本配对股票价格数据集。实验结果表明，语义和多层次配对策略在股价预测中有效。将方法应用于专有新闻源可获得更高质量的配对数据和更好的股价预测性能。

Conclusion: 提出的语义和多层次配对框架能够更好地捕捉金融市场的复杂依赖关系，构建高质量的文本配对时间序列数据集，显著提升股价预测性能，为金融时间序列分析提供更有效的数据基础。

Abstract: The financial domain involves a variety of important time-series problems. Recently, time-series analysis methods that jointly leverage textual and numerical information have gained increasing attention. Accordingly, numerous efforts have been made to construct text-paired time-series datasets in the financial domain. However, financial markets are characterized by complex interdependencies, in which a company's stock price is influenced not only by company-specific events but also by events in other companies and broader macroeconomic factors. Existing approaches that pair text with financial time-series data based on simple keyword matching often fail to capture such complex relationships. To address this limitation, we propose a semantic-based and multi-level pairing framework. Specifically, we extract company-specific context for the target company from SEC filings and apply an embedding-based matching mechanism to retrieve semantically relevant news articles based on this context. Furthermore, we classify news articles into four levels (macro-level, sector-level, related company-level, and target-company level) using large language models (LLMs), enabling multi-level pairing of news articles with the target company. Applying this framework to publicly-available news datasets, we construct \textbf{FinTexTS}, a new large-scale text-paired stock price dataset. Experimental results on \textbf{FinTexTS} demonstrate the effectiveness of our semantic-based and multi-level pairing strategy in stock price forecasting. In addition to publicly-available news underlying \textbf{FinTexTS}, we show that applying our method to proprietary yet carefully curated news sources leads to higher-quality paired data and improved stock price forecasting performance.

</details>


### [218] [A Natural Language Agentic Approach to Study Affective Polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02711)
*Stephanie Anneris Malvicini,Ewelina Gajewska,Arda Derbent,Katarzyna Budzynska,Jarosław A. Chudziak,Maria Vanina Martinez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于多智能体模型和LLM的社交平台，用于研究社交媒体中的情感极化现象，克服传统研究的数据限制和主观偏差问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感极化研究存在局限性：真实世界研究范围有限，模拟研究缺乏高质量训练数据，手动标注成本高且主观性强，缺乏统一工具来形式化不同定义，阻碍结果比较和互操作框架发展

Method: 开发多智能体模型平台，利用大语言模型构建虚拟社区，智能体在社区中进行讨论互动，支持不同粒度和抽象层次的情感极化观测与测量

Result: 平台成为研究复杂社会动态（如情感极化）的灵活计算工具，能够模拟丰富、上下文敏感的互动，系统探索传统依赖人类受试者研究的问题

Conclusion: 该多智能体平台为社交媒体情感极化研究提供了全面方法，通过LLM驱动的虚拟社区实现了对社会科学文献中相关问题的分析，支持不同场景下的极化观测，是计算社会科学研究的有力工具

Abstract: Affective polarization has been central to political and social studies, with growing focus on social media, where partisan divisions are often exacerbated. Real-world studies tend to have limited scope, while simulated studies suffer from insufficient high-quality training data, as manually labeling posts is labor-intensive and prone to subjective biases. The lack of adequate tools to formalize different definitions of affective polarization across studies complicates result comparison and hinders interoperable frameworks. We present a multi-agent model providing a comprehensive approach to studying affective polarization in social media. To operationalize our framework, we develop a platform leveraging large language models (LLMs) to construct virtual communities where agents engage in discussions. We showcase the potential of our platform by (1) analyzing questions related to affective polarization, as explored in social science literature, providing a fresh perspective on this phenomenon, and (2) introducing scenarios that allow observation and measurement of polarization at different levels of granularity and abstraction. Experiments show that our platform is a flexible tool for computational studies of complex social dynamics such as affective polarization. It leverages advanced agent models to simulate rich, context-sensitive interactions and systematically explore research questions traditionally addressed through human-subject studies.

</details>


### [219] [Rethinking Code Similarity for Automated Algorithm Design with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02787)
*Rui Zhang,Zhichao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BehaveSim：一种通过问题解决行为轨迹衡量算法相似性的新方法，用于区分真正的算法创新与表面语法变化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型的自动算法设计（LLM-AAD）的兴起，算法的主要设计原则往往隐含在生成的代码中。现有代码相似性度量方法无法捕捉算法相似性，因为它们关注表面语法或输出等价性，而非底层算法逻辑。

Method: 提出BehaveSim方法，通过问题解决行为轨迹（PSTrajs）衡量算法相似性。PSTrajs是算法执行过程中产生的中间解序列。使用动态时间规整（DTW）量化PSTrajs之间的对齐度，从而区分具有不同逻辑但语法或输出相似的算法。

Result: 在两个关键应用中展示了BehaveSim的实用性：1）增强LLM-AAD：将BehaveSim集成到现有LLM-AAD框架（如FunSearch、EoH）中，促进行为多样性，显著提高了三个AAD任务的性能；2）算法分析：通过行为对生成的算法进行聚类，实现对问题解决策略的系统分析。

Conclusion: BehaveSim为衡量算法相似性提供了新视角，通过问题解决行为轨迹有效区分算法逻辑差异。该方法对于LLM-AAD框架的改进和AI生成算法的系统分析具有重要意义，相关数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: The rise of Large Language Model-based Automated Algorithm Design (LLM-AAD) has transformed algorithm development by autonomously generating code implementations of expert-level algorithms. Unlike traditional expert-driven algorithm development, in the LLM-AAD paradigm, the main design principle behind an algorithm is often implicitly embedded in the generated code. Therefore, assessing algorithmic similarity directly from code, distinguishing genuine algorithmic innovation from mere syntactic variation, becomes essential. While various code similarity metrics exist, they fail to capture algorithmic similarity, as they focus on surface-level syntax or output equivalence rather than the underlying algorithmic logic.
  We propose BehaveSim, a novel method to measure algorithmic similarity through the lens of problem-solving behavior as a sequence of intermediate solutions produced during execution, dubbed as problem-solving trajectories (PSTrajs). By quantifying the alignment between PSTrajs using dynamic time warping (DTW), BehaveSim distinguishes algorithms with divergent logic despite syntactic or output-level similarities. We demonstrate its utility in two key applications: (i) Enhancing LLM-AAD: Integrating BehaveSim into existing LLM-AAD frameworks (e.g., FunSearch, EoH) promotes behavioral diversity, significantly improving performance on three AAD tasks. (ii) Algorithm analysis: BehaveSim clusters generated algorithms by behavior, enabling systematic analysis of problem-solving strategies--a crucial tool for the growing ecosystem of AI-generated algorithms. Data and code of this work are open-sourced at https://github.com/RayZhhh/behavesim.

</details>


### [220] [LLM-based Argument Mining meets Argumentation and Description Logics: a Unified Framework for Reasoning about Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02858)
*Gianvincenzo Alfano,Sergio Greco,Lucio La Cava,Stefano Francesco Monea,Irina Trubitsyna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个结合学习型论证挖掘、定量推理和本体查询的框架，用于分析辩论文本，克服LLM在复杂文本推理上的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在分析和生成文本方面表现良好，但在复杂辩论文本的显式、透明和可验证推理方面存在困难，缺乏捕捉论证间支持/攻击关系及其相对强度如何决定整体可接受性的结构化表示

Method: 从原始辩论文本开始，提取模糊论证知识库，将论证表示为实体，通过攻击和支持关系链接，并用反映在辩论上下文中合理性的初始模糊强度进行标注。然后应用定量论证语义学，通过传播支持和攻击的影响来计算最终论证强度。最后将这些结果嵌入模糊描述逻辑设置中，通过高效重写技术实现表达性查询回答

Result: 该方法为分析辩论提供了透明、可解释且形式化基础的方法，克服了基于LLM的纯统计分析方法的局限性

Conclusion: 提出的框架通过整合学习型论证挖掘、定量推理和本体查询，为复杂辩论文本的分析提供了更结构化、透明和可验证的推理方法，超越了传统LLM方法的局限性

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance in analyzing and generating text, yet they struggle with explicit, transparent, and verifiable reasoning over complex texts such as those containing debates. In particular, they lack structured representations that capture how arguments support or attack each other and how their relative strengths determine overall acceptability. We encompass these limitations by proposing a framework that integrates learning-based argument mining with quantitative reasoning and ontology-based querying. Starting from a raw debate text, the framework extracts a fuzzy argumentative knowledge base, where arguments are explicitly represented as entities, linked by attack and support relations, and annotated with initial fuzzy strengths reflecting plausibility w.r.t. the debate's context. Quantitative argumentation semantics are then applied to compute final argument strengths by propagating the effects of supports and attacks. These results are then embedded into a fuzzy description logic setting, enabling expressive query answering through efficient rewriting techniques. The proposed approach provides a transparent, explainable, and formally grounded method for analyzing debates, overcoming purely statistical LLM-based analyses.

</details>


### [221] [SAE as a Crystal Ball: Interpretable Features Predict Cross-domain Transferability of LLMs without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02908)
*Qi Zhang,Yifei Wang,Xiaohan Wang,Jiajun Chai,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于稀疏自编码器的可迁移性评分（STS），用于预测大语言模型微调后的跨域性能变化，无需实际微调即可评估模型迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在下游任务中的效果不仅取决于预训练，还受到后训练（如微调）过程的影响。后训练会引入模型偏移，这些偏移如何影响不同领域的性能转移尚不清楚。需要一种能够在微调前预测模型可迁移性的方法。

Method: 提出SAE-based Transferability Score (STS)方法：使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）识别模型表示中的偏移维度，计算这些维度与下游领域的相关性，从而在微调前预测模型的可迁移性。以监督微调为例进行验证，并初步扩展到强化学习。

Result: 在多个模型和领域上的实验表明，STS能够准确预测监督微调的可迁移性，与实际性能变化的皮尔逊相关系数超过0.7。该方法为后训练策略提供了可解释的指导工具。

Conclusion: STS是一种有效的可迁移性预测指标，能够在大语言模型后训练前评估模型在不同领域的性能转移潜力。该方法具有可解释性，可为后训练策略提供指导，代码已开源。

Abstract: In recent years, pre-trained large language models have achieved remarkable success across diverse tasks. Besides the pivotal role of self-supervised pre-training, their effectiveness in downstream applications also depends critically on the post-training process, which adapts models to task-specific data and objectives. However, this process inevitably introduces model shifts that can influence performance in different domains, and how such shifts transfer remains poorly understood. To open up the black box, we propose the SAE-based Transferability Score (STS), a new metric that leverages sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to forecast post-training transferability. Taking supervised fine-tuning as an example, STS identifies shifted dimensions in SAE representations and calculates their correlations with downstream domains, enabling reliable estimation of transferability \textit{before} fine-tuning. Extensive experiments across multiple models and domains show that STS accurately predicts the transferability of supervised fine-tuning, achieving Pearson correlation coefficients above 0.7 with actual performance changes. Beyond this, we take an initial step toward extending STS to reinforcement learning. We believe that STS can serve as an {\color{black} interpretable} tool for guiding post-training strategies in LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/PKU-ML/STS.

</details>


### [222] [ShipTraj-R1: Reinforcing Ship Trajectory Prediction in Large Language Models via Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02939)
*Yang Zhan,Yunhao Li,Zhang Chao,Yuxu Lu,Yan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ShipTraj-R1：基于大语言模型的船舶轨迹预测框架，通过GRPO强化微调实现自适应思维链推理，在复杂海事数据集上优于现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然强化微调显著提升了大语言模型的推理能力，但将LLMs应用于船舶轨迹预测领域仍未被充分探索。现有方法在该特定领域存在局限性，需要专门设计的框架来解决这一实际问题。

Method: 1) 设计包含冲突船舶轨迹信息的动态提示，引导模型实现自适应思维链推理；2) 引入全面的基于规则的奖励机制，激励模型的推理格式和预测准确性；3) 通过GRPO机制在领域特定提示和奖励指导下进行强化，使用Qwen3作为模型主干

Result: 在两个复杂真实世界海事数据集上的广泛实验表明，ShipTraj-R1相比最先进的深度学习和基于LLM的基线方法实现了最小误差

Conclusion: ShipTraj-R1成功将船舶轨迹预测重新定义为文本到文本生成问题，通过创新的提示设计和奖励机制，结合GRPO强化微调，在船舶轨迹预测任务上取得了优异性能

Abstract: Recent advancements in reinforcement fine-tuning have significantly improved the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs). In particular, methods such as group relative policy optimization (GRPO) have demonstrated strong capabilities across various fields. However, applying LLMs to ship trajectory prediction remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we propose ShipTraj-R1, a novel LLM-based framework that reformulates ship trajectory prediction as a text-to-text generation problem. (1) We design a dynamic prompt containing trajectory information about conflicting ships to guide the model to achieve adaptive chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning. (2) We introduce a comprehensive rule-based reward mechanism to incentivize the reasoning format and prediction accuracy of the model. (3) Our ShipTraj-R1 is reinforced through the GRPO mechanism guided by domain-specific prompts and rewards, and utilizes the Qwen3 as the model backbone. Extensive experimental results on two complex and real-world maritime datasets show that the proposed ShipTraj-R1 achieves the least error compared with state-of-the-art deep learning and LLM-based baselines.

</details>


### [223] [SpatialText: A Pure-Text Cognitive Benchmark for Spatial Understanding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03002)
*Peiyao Jiang,Zequn Qin,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SpatialText框架，通过双源方法分离文本空间推理，揭示当前大语言模型依赖语言共现启发式而非构建连贯内部空间表征的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法将真正的空间认知与统计语言启发式分离，多模态评估常混淆空间推理与视觉感知，需要系统研究模型是否构建灵活的空间心理模型。

Method: 提出SpatialText诊断框架，采用双源方法：整合人工标注的真实3D室内环境描述（捕捉自然模糊性、视角转换和功能关系）与代码生成的逻辑精确场景（探测形式空间推理和认知边界）。

Result: 模型在检索显式空间事实和全局坐标系操作方面表现良好，但在自我中心视角转换和局部参考系推理方面存在系统性失败，表明其依赖语言共现启发式而非构建可验证的内部空间表征。

Conclusion: SpatialText作为诊断人工空间智能认知边界的严格工具，揭示了当前模型缺乏构建连贯心理模型的能力，为未来空间推理研究提供了理论驱动的评估框架。

Abstract: Genuine spatial reasoning relies on the capacity to construct and manipulate coherent internal spatial representations, often conceptualized as mental models, rather than merely processing surface linguistic associations. While large language models exhibit advanced capabilities across various domains, existing benchmarks fail to isolate this intrinsic spatial cognition from statistical language heuristics. Furthermore, multimodal evaluations frequently conflate genuine spatial reasoning with visual perception. To systematically investigate whether models construct flexible spatial mental models, we introduce SpatialText, a theory-driven diagnostic framework. Rather than functioning simply as a dataset, SpatialText isolates text-based spatial reasoning through a dual-source methodology. It integrates human-annotated descriptions of real 3D indoor environments, which capture natural ambiguities, perspective shifts, and functional relations, with code-generated, logically precise scenes designed to probe formal spatial deduction and epistemic boundaries. Systematic evaluation across state-of-the-art models reveals fundamental representational limitations. Although models demonstrate proficiency in retrieving explicit spatial facts and operating within global, allocentric coordinate systems, they exhibit critical failures in egocentric perspective transformation and local reference frame reasoning. These systematic errors provide strong evidence that current models rely heavily on linguistic co-occurrence heuristics rather than constructing coherent, verifiable internal spatial representations. SpatialText thus serves as a rigorous instrument for diagnosing the cognitive boundaries of artificial spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [224] [OrchMAS: Orchestrated Reasoning with Multi Collaborative Heterogeneous Scientific Expert Structured Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03005)
*Yichao Feng,Haoran Luo,Zhenghong Lin,Yiqun Sun,Pengfei Wei,Lawrence B. Hsieh,Anh Tuan Luu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向科学领域的两层多模型编排框架，通过动态构建领域感知推理管道和迭代更新机制，解决现有多智能体LLM系统在科学知识密集型任务中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体大语言模型框架在科学和知识密集型领域表现不佳，主要问题包括：静态提示和智能体角色、僵化的工作流程、同质化模型依赖，导致领域适应能力差、推理灵活性有限、异构或长时程科学任务延迟高，且难以修正早期决策错误。

Method: 提出科学领域导向的交互式两层多模型编排框架：1) 编排模型分析任务，动态构建领域感知推理管道，实例化具有定制提示的专家智能体；2) 执行模型在生成的角色和指令规范下执行每个步骤。编排器基于中间反馈迭代更新管道，实现动态重新规划、角色重新分配和提示优化。

Result: 实验显示，该框架在多样化的推理和科学风格基准测试中，相比现有多智能体系统和强基线模型取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结构化异构模型协作增强了科学推理的鲁棒性和专业化，具有模型无关性，支持不同能力或成本的异构LLM集成，能够在实际科学部署中实现灵活的性能效率权衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model frameworks are promising for complex multi step reasoning, yet existing systems remain weak for scientific and knowledge intensive domains due to static prompts and agent roles, rigid workflows, and homogeneous model reliance, leading to poor domain adaptation, limited reasoning flexibility, and high latency on heterogeneous or long-horizon scientific tasks. They also struggle to revise earlier decisions when intermediate reasoning diverges, reducing reliability in structured and calculation heavy settings. To address these limitations, we propose a scientific domain oriented interactive two tier multi model orchestration framework. A dedicated orchestration model analyzes each task, dynamically constructs a domain aware reasoning pipeline, and instantiates specialized expert agents with tailored prompts, while an execution model performs each step under generated role and instruction specifications. The orchestrator iteratively updates the pipeline based on intermediate feedback, enabling dynamic replanning, role reallocation, and prompt refinement across multi turn interactions, strengthening robustness and specialization for scientific reasoning through structured heterogeneous model collaboration. The framework is model agnostic and supports heterogeneous LLM integration with different capacities or costs, enabling flexible performance efficiency trade offs in practical scientific deployments. Experiments show consistent improvements over existing multi agent systems and strong baselines across diverse reasoning and scientific style benchmarks.

</details>


### [225] [REGAL: A Registry-Driven Architecture for Deterministic Grounding of Agentic AI in Enterprise Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03018)
*Yuvraj Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REGAL提出了一种基于注册表的架构，用于在企业遥测数据上确定性地基础化智能体AI系统，通过将确定性遥测计算作为一等原语，并让LLM在受控、版本化的操作空间上运行，而非原始事件流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业工程组织产生大量异构遥测数据，LLM虽能实现新型智能体自动化，但在私有遥测数据上基础化这些智能体面临三个实际挑战：有限的模型上下文、本地定义的语义概念以及不断演化的指标接口。

Method: REGAL采用明确的架构方法：1) Medallion ELT管道生成可重放、语义压缩的Gold工件；2) 基于注册表的编译层从声明性指标定义合成模型上下文协议(MCP)工具。注册表作为"接口即代码"层，确保工具规范与执行的一致性，缓解工具漂移，并在语义边界直接嵌入治理策略。

Result: 原型实现和案例研究验证了确定性基础化的可行性，并展示了其对延迟、令牌效率和操作治理的影响。该工作系统化了企业LLM基础化的架构模式。

Conclusion: REGAL将确定性计算和语义编译提升为智能体系统的一等设计原语，而非提出新的学习算法。该架构通过注册表驱动的方法解决了企业遥测数据上基础化LLM智能体的实际挑战。

Abstract: Enterprise engineering organizations produce high-volume, heterogeneous telemetry from version control systems, CI/CD pipelines, issue trackers, and observability platforms. Large Language Models (LLMs) enable new forms of agentic automation, but grounding such agents on private telemetry raises three practical challenges: limited model context, locally defined semantic concepts, and evolving metric interfaces.
  We present REGAL, a registry-driven architecture for deterministic grounding of agentic AI systems in enterprise telemetry. REGAL adopts an explicitly architectural approach: deterministic telemetry computation is treated as a first-class primitive, and LLMs operate over a bounded, version-controlled action space rather than raw event streams.
  The architecture combines (1) a Medallion ELT pipeline that produces replayable, semantically compressed Gold artifacts, and (2) a registry-driven compilation layer that synthesizes Model Context Protocol (MCP) tools from declarative metric definitions. The registry functions as an "interface-as-code" layer, ensuring alignment between tool specification and execution, mitigating tool drift, and embedding governance policies directly at the semantic boundary.
  A prototype implementation and case study validate the feasibility of deterministic grounding and illustrate its implications for latency, token efficiency, and operational governance. This work systematizes an architectural pattern for enterprise LLM grounding; it does not propose new learning algorithms, but rather elevates deterministic computation and semantic compilation to first-class design primitives for agentic systems.

</details>


### [226] [TikZilla: Scaling Text-to-TikZ with High-Quality Data and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03072)
*Christian Greisinger,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TikZilla模型，通过构建大规模高质量DaTikZ-V4数据集和两阶段训练（SFT+RL），使用图像编码器提供语义奖励，显著提升了文本到TikZ代码生成的质量，在人类评估中超越GPT-4o并匹配GPT-5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到TikZ生成面临两个主要问题：1）现有数据集规模小、噪声大，无法捕捉TikZ的复杂性，导致文本与渲染图像不匹配；2）现有方法仅依赖监督微调（SFT），模型无法接触渲染图像的语义信息，导致循环、无关内容、空间关系错误等问题。

Method: 1）构建DaTikZ-V4数据集，规模是DaTikZ-V3的四倍以上，质量更高，包含LLM生成的图像描述；2）训练TikZilla模型家族（3B和8B参数），采用两阶段训练流程：先进行监督微调（SFT），再进行强化学习（RL）；3）RL阶段使用通过逆向图形训练的图像编码器提供语义忠实的奖励信号。

Result: 经过超过1000次判断的人类评估显示：TikZilla在5分量表上比基础模型提升1.5-2分，超越GPT-4o 0.5分，在基于图像的评估中与GPT-5表现相当，同时模型规模远小于这些大型模型。

Conclusion: 通过构建大规模高质量数据集和结合SFT与RL的两阶段训练方法，TikZilla显著提升了文本到TikZ代码生成的质量，在较小模型规模下达到了与大型商业模型相当甚至更好的性能，为科学图像生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to assist scientists across diverse workflows. A key challenge is generating high-quality figures from textual descriptions, often represented as TikZ programs that can be rendered as scientific images. Prior research has proposed a variety of datasets and modeling approaches for this task. However, existing datasets for Text-to-TikZ are too small and noisy to capture the complexity of TikZ, causing mismatches between text and rendered figures. Moreover, prior approaches rely solely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which does not expose the model to the rendered semantics of the figure, often resulting in errors such as looping, irrelevant content, and incorrect spatial relations. To address these issues, we construct DaTikZ-V4, a dataset more than four times larger and substantially higher in quality than DaTikZ-V3, enriched with LLM-generated figure descriptions. Using this dataset, we train TikZilla, a family of small open-source Qwen models (3B and 8B) with a two-stage pipeline of SFT followed by reinforcement learning (RL). For RL, we leverage an image encoder trained via inverse graphics to provide semantically faithful reward signals. Extensive human evaluations with over 1,000 judgments show that TikZilla improves by 1.5-2 points over its base models on a 5-point scale, surpasses GPT-4o by 0.5 points, and matches GPT-5 in the image-based evaluation, while operating at much smaller model sizes. Code, data, and models will be made available.

</details>


### [227] [RAPO: Expanding Exploration for LLM Agents via Retrieval-Augmented Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03078)
*Siwei Zhang,Yun Xiong,Xi Chen,Zi'an Jia,Renhong Huang,Jiarong Xu,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAPO提出检索增强的策略优化框架，通过混合策略的智能体展开和检索感知的策略优化，解决现有Agentic RL中探索受限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic RL方法主要依赖纯在线策略范式进行探索，限制了智能体只能基于自身生成的输出进行探索，无法发现新的推理视角。虽然近期研究尝试引入辅助离线策略信号，但它们通常使用完整的离线轨迹进行轨迹级策略估计，忽略了智能体展开过程中细粒度、步骤级的探索动态需求。

Method: 提出检索增强的策略优化（RAPO）框架，将训练过程分解为两个阶段：1）混合策略的智能体展开，允许智能体在检索到的离线步骤级轨迹上进行连续推理，动态扩展推理感受野；2）检索感知的策略优化，通过检索奖励和重要性塑形来校准策略梯度估计，稳定训练并优先考虑检索启发的探索。

Result: 在三个智能体推理任务的十四个数据集上，RAPO实现了平均+5.0%的性能提升，同时训练效率提高了1.2倍。

Conclusion: RAPO通过引入检索机制显式扩展Agentic RL中的探索，解决了现有方法的局限性，在性能和训练效率方面均取得了显著改进。

Abstract: Agentic Reinforcement Learning (Agentic RL) has shown remarkable potential in large language model-based (LLM) agents. These works can empower LLM agents to tackle complex tasks via multi-step, tool-integrated reasoning. However, an inherent limitation of existing Agentic RL methods is their reliance on a pure on-policy paradigm for exploration, restricting exploration to the agent's self-generated outputs and preventing the discovery of new reasoning perspectives for further improvement. While recent efforts incorporate auxiliary off-policy signals to enhance exploration, they typically utilize full off-policy trajectories for trajectory-level policy estimation, overlooking the necessity for the fine-grained, step-level exploratory dynamics within agentic rollout. In this paper, we revisit exploration in Agentic RL and propose Retrieval-Augmented Policy Optimization (RAPO), a novel RL framework that introduces retrieval to explicitly expand exploration during training. To achieve this, we decompose the Agentic RL training process into two phases: (i) Hybrid-policy Agentic Rollout, and (ii) Retrieval-aware Policy Optimization. Specifically, we propose a Hybrid-policy Agentic Rollout strategy, which allows the agents to continuously reason over the retrieved off-policy step-level traces. It dynamically extends the reasoning receptive field of agents, enabling broader exploration conditioned on external behaviors. Subsequently, we introduce the Retrieval-aware Policy Optimization mechanism, which calibrates the policy gradient estimation with retrieval reward and importance shaping, stabilizing training and prioritizing retrieval-illuminating exploration. Extensive experiments show that RAPO achieves an +5.0% average gain on fourteen datasets across three agentic reasoning tasks, while delivering 1.2x faster training efficiency.

</details>


### [228] [Beyond Factual Correctness: Mitigating Preference-Inconsistent Explanations in Explainable Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03080)
*Chengkai Wang,Baisong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PURE框架通过选择与用户偏好一致的多跳推理路径，解决LLM推荐解释中偏好不一致问题，在保持推荐准确性的同时减少偏好冲突解释


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统生成的解释虽然事实正确，但常使用与用户历史偏好冲突的属性进行推荐理由，产生逻辑有效但缺乏说服力的解释，而标准幻觉或忠实度指标无法检测这种偏好不一致问题

Method: 提出PURE偏好感知推理框架，采用选择-生成范式：1) 在证据选择阶段，基于用户意图、特异性和多样性选择事实基础且与用户偏好结构对齐的紧凑多跳项目中心推理路径；2) 通过结构感知提示将选定证据注入LLM生成，保持关系约束

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，PURE能持续减少偏好不一致解释和事实幻觉，同时保持竞争力的推荐准确性、解释质量和推理效率

Conclusion: 可信的解释不仅需要事实正确性，还需要与用户偏好对齐的合理性，PURE通过干预证据选择而非仅改进生成过程，有效解决了偏好不一致问题

Abstract: LLM-based explainable recommenders can produce fluent explanations that are factually correct, yet still justify items using attributes that conflict with a user's historical preferences. Such preference-inconsistent explanations yield logically valid but unconvincing reasoning and are largely missed by standard hallucination or faithfulness metrics. We formalize this failure mode and propose PURE, a preference-aware reasoning framework following a select-then-generate paradigm. Instead of only improving generation, PURE intervenes in evidence selection, it selects a compact set of multi-hop item-centric reasoning paths that are both factually grounded and aligned with user preference structure, guided by user intent, specificity, and diversity to suppress generic, weakly personalized evidence. The selected evidence is then injected into LLM generation via structure-aware prompting that preserves relational constraints. To measure preference inconsistency, we introduce a feature-level, user-centric evaluation metric that reveals misalignment overlooked by factuality-based measures. Experiments on three real-world datasets show that PURE consistently reduces preference-inconsistent explanations and factual hallucinations while maintaining competitive recommendation accuracy, explanation quality, and inference efficiency. These results highlight that trustworthy explanations require not only factual correctness but also justification aligned with user preferences.

</details>


### [229] [Odin: Multi-Signal Graph Intelligence for Autonomous Discovery in Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03097)
*Muyukani Kizito,Elizabeth Nyambere*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Odin是首个生产部署的图智能引擎，用于在知识图谱中自主发现有意义模式，无需预先指定。它通过COMPASS评分（结合结构重要性、语义合理性、时间相关性和社区感知指导）引导探索，解决了"回声室"问题，在医疗和保险等受监管环境中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索的系统只能回答预定义查询，无法自主发现知识图谱中的有意义模式。受监管行业需要可追溯、无幻觉的自主发现系统，但现有方法存在"回声室"问题，探索易被困在密集局部社区中。

Method: 提出COMPASS评分：1) 结构重要性（个性化PageRank）；2) 语义合理性（NPLL作为判别式过滤器）；3) 时间相关性（可配置衰减）；4) 社区感知指导（GNN识别桥接实体和社区间亲和度）。使用波束搜索实现O(b·h)复杂度，保持高召回率。

Result: Odin是首个在受监管生产环境（医疗和保险）部署的自主发现系统，显著提升了模式发现质量和分析师效率。系统保持完整的可追溯性，满足监管要求，避免了幻觉问题。

Conclusion: Odin通过多信号集成（特别是桥接评分机制）有效解决了图探索中的"回声室"问题，实现了高效、可追溯的自主模式发现，为受监管行业的知识图谱分析提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We present Odin, the first production-deployed graph intelligence engine for autonomous discovery of meaningful patterns in knowledge graphs without prior specification. Unlike retrieval-based systems that answer predefined queries, Odin guides exploration through the COMPASS (Composite Oriented Multi-signal Path Assessment) score, a novel metric that combines (1) structural importance via Personalized PageRank, (2) semantic plausibility through Neural Probabilistic Logic Learning (NPLL) used as a discriminative filter rather than generative model, (3) temporal relevance with configurable decay, and (4) community-aware guidance through GNN-identified bridge entities and inter-community affinity scores. This multi-signal integration, particularly the bridge scoring mechanism, addresses the "echo chamber" problem where graph exploration becomes trapped in dense local communities. We formalize the autonomous discovery problem, prove theoretical properties of our scoring function, and demonstrate that beam search with multi-signal guidance achieves $O(b \cdot h)$ complexity while maintaining high recall compared to exhaustive exploration. To our knowledge, Odin represents the first autonomous discovery system deployed in regulated production environments (healthcare and insurance), demonstrating significant improvements in pattern discovery quality and analyst efficiency. Our approach maintains complete provenance traceability -- a critical requirement for regulated industries where hallucination is unacceptable.
